import type { Metadata } from 'next'
import { Plus_Jakarta_Sans } from 'next/font/google'
import './globals.css'
import { ThemeProvider } from '@/context/ThemeContext'
import ThemeBackground from '@/components/ThemeBackground'
import Footer from '@/components/Footer'
import FocusModeButton from '@/components/FocusModeButton'

const plusJakartaSans = Plus_Jakarta_Sans({
  subsets: ['latin'],
  display: 'swap',
  variable: '--font-plus-jakarta-sans',
})

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'CBT SD NEGERI GETASAN',
  description: 'Platform Ujian CBT Interaktif SD Negeri Getasan',
}

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode
}) {
  return (
    <html lang="id">
      <body
        className={`${plusJakartaSans.className} antialiased min-h-screen flex flex-col justify-between relative`}
      >
        <ThemeProvider>
          <ThemeBackground />
          <div className="relative z-10 flex-grow">{children}</div>
          <Footer />
          <FocusModeButton />
        </ThemeProvider>
      </body>
    </html>
  )
}

FILE: components/editor/EditorToolbar.tsx

'use client'

import type { Editor } from '@tiptap/react'

interface EditorToolbarProps {
  editor: Editor | null
}

export default function EditorToolbar({ editor }: EditorToolbarProps) {
  if (!editor) return null

  const buttonClass = (active = false) =>
    `rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 text-sm transition ${
      active
        ? 'border-blue-500 bg-blue-100 text-blue-700'
        : 'border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100'
    }`

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-3">
      <button
        type="button"
        title="Tebal"
        className={buttonClass(editor.isActive('bold'))}
        onMouseDown={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleBold().run()}
      >
        <strong>B</strong>
      </button>

      <button
        type="button"
        title="Miring"
        className={buttonClass(editor.isActive('italic'))}
        onMouseDown={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleItalic().run()}
      >
        <em>I</em>
      </button>

      <button
        type="button"
        title="Coret"
        className={buttonClass(editor.isActive('strike'))}
        onMouseDown={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleStrike().run()}
      >
        <s>S</s>
      </button>

      <span className="mx-1 h-6 border-l border-slate-300" />

      <button
        type="button"
        className={buttonClass(editor.isActive('heading', { level: 2 }))}
        onMouseDown={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
        onClick={() =>
          editor.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level: 2 }).run()
        }
      >
        Judul
      </button>

      <button
        type="button"
        className={buttonClass(editor.isActive('paragraph'))}
        onMouseDown={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setParagraph().run()}
      >
        Paragraf
      </button>

      <span className="mx-1 h-6 border-l border-slate-300" />

      <button
        type="button"
        className={buttonClass(editor.isActive('bulletList'))}
        onMouseDown={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleBulletList().run()}
      >
        • Daftar
      </button>

      <button
        type="button"
        className={buttonClass(editor.isActive('orderedList'))}
        onMouseDown={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleOrderedList().run()}
      >
        1. Daftar
      </button>

      <span className="mx-1 h-6 border-l border-slate-300" />

      <button
        type="button"
        title="Urungkan"
        className={buttonClass()}
        disabled={!editor.can().undo()}
        onMouseDown={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().undo().run()}
      >
        ↶
      </button>

      <button
        type="button"
        title="Ulangi"
        className={buttonClass()}
        disabled={!editor.can().redo()}
        onMouseDown={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
        onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().redo().run()}
      >
        ↷
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}



====================================================================================================
FILE: components/editor/RichTextEditor.tsx
====================================================================================================

'use client'

import { useEditor, EditorContent } from '@tiptap/react'
import StarterKit from '@tiptap/starter-kit'
import EditorToolbar from './EditorToolbar'
import TextAlign from '@tiptap/extension-text-align'

interface RichTextEditorProps {
  content?: string
  onChange?: (html: string) => void
  placeholder?: string
  editable?: boolean
}

export default function RichTextEditor({
  content = '',
  onChange,
  placeholder = 'Tuliskan teks di sini...',
  editable = true,
}: RichTextEditorProps) {
  const editor = useEditor({
    extensions: [
        StarterKit,
        TextAlign.configure({
            types: ['heading', 'paragraph'],
        }),
        ],
    content,
    immediatelyRender: false,
    editable,
    editorProps: {
      attributes: {
        class:
          'min-h-[180px] w-full p-4 outline-none text-slate-800 leading-relaxed',
        'data-placeholder': placeholder,
      },
    },
    onUpdate: ({ editor }) => {
      onChange?.(editor.getHTML())
    },
  })

  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white">
      <EditorToolbar editor={editor} />
      <EditorContent editor={editor} />
    </div>
  )
}



====================================================================================================
FILE: components/MathRenderer.tsx
====================================================================================================

'use client'

import React from 'react'
import katex from 'katex'
import 'katex/dist/katex.min.css'

interface MathRendererProps {
  content: string
  className?: string
}

export default function MathRenderer({
  content,
  className = '',
}: MathRendererProps) {
  if (!content) return null

  const renderFormattedContent = (text: string) => {
    try {
      // Pisahkan teks biasa dan rumus KaTeX.
      const parts = text.split(/(\$\$[\s\S]*?\$\$|\$[\s\S]*?\$)/g)

      const processedParts = parts.map((part) => {
        // Rumus blok.
        if (part.startsWith('$$') && part.endsWith('$$')) {
          const math = part.slice(2, -2)

          return katex.renderToString(math, {
            displayMode: true,
            throwOnError: false,
          })
        }

        // Rumus dalam satu baris.
        if (part.startsWith('$') && part.endsWith('$')) {
          const math = part.slice(1, -1)

          return katex.renderToString(math, {
            displayMode: false,
            throwOnError: false,
          })
        }

        // Format teks tebal dan miring.
        let formatted = part
          .replace(
            /\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g,
            '<strong style="font-weight: 700;">$1</strong>'
          )
          .replace(
            /\*(.*?)\*/g,
            '<em style="font-style: italic;">$1</em>'
          )

        // Pertahankan baris baru.
        if (!/<table|<blockquote|<ul|<ol/i.test(formatted)) {
          formatted = formatted.replace(/\n/g, '<br />')
        }

        return formatted
      })

      return processedParts.join('')
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('MathRenderer Error:', err)
      return text
    }
  }

  const htmlContent = renderFormattedContent(content)

  return (
    <div
      dir="auto"
      className={`
        math-rendered-content min-w-0 text-inherit
        [&_*]:!text-inherit
        [&_strong]:font-bold
        [&_b]:font-bold
        [&_em]:italic
        [&_i]:italic
        [&_u]:underline

        [&_[dir=rtl]]:text-right
        [&_[dir=rtl]]:leading-loose
        [&_[dir=rtl]]:unicode-bidi-plaintext

        [&_.aksara-jawa]:font-aksara-jawa
        [&_.aksara-jawa]:leading-loose

        [&_.katex]:!text-inherit
        [&_.katex-display]:overflow-x-auto
        [&_.katex-display]:overflow-y-hidden

        [&_table]:my-3
        [&_table]:w-full
        [&_table]:border-collapse
        [&_table]:border
        [&_table]:border-slate-300
        [&_table]:bg-white
        [&_table]:!text-slate-800
        [&_th]:border
        [&_th]:border-slate-300
        [&_th]:bg-slate-100
        [&_th]:p-2
        [&_th]:!text-slate-800
        [&_th]:font-bold
        [&_td]:border
        [&_td]:border-slate-300
        [&_td]:p-2
        [&_td]:!text-slate-800
        [&_blockquote]:my-2
        [&_blockquote]:border-l-4
        [&_blockquote]:border-indigo-500
        [&_blockquote]:bg-slate-50
        [&_blockquote]:py-1
        [&_blockquote]:pl-3
        [&_blockquote]:italic

        ${className}
      `}
      style={{
        color: 'inherit',
        unicodeBidi: 'plaintext',
      }}
      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: htmlContent }}
    />
  )
}

FILE: app/admin/exams/[id]/questions/page.tsx

'use client'

import { useEffect, useState, Suspense } from 'react'
import { useParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'
import MathRenderer from '@/components/MathRenderer'
import Link from 'next/link'

function BankSoalContent() {
  const params = useParams()
  const examId = params?.id as string
  const router = useRouter()

  const [exam, setExam] = useState<any>(null)
  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState<any[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  // State Modal Form Editor Manual
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false)
  const [editingQuestion, setEditingQuestion] = useState<any>(null)

  // State Modal Import Bulk
  const [isBulkModalOpen, setIsBulkModalOpen] = useState(false)
  const [bulkInputText, setBulkInputText] = useState('')

  // State Form Input Manual
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    question_text: '',
    option_a: '',
    option_b: '',
    option_c: '',
    option_d: '',
    correct_option: 'A',
    explanation: '',
    image_url: '',
    target_x: 3,
    target_y: 3,
  })

  // 🟢 1. FETCH DATA EXAM & SOAL
  const fetchExamAndQuestions = async () => {
    if (!examId) return
    setLoading(true)
    const supabase = createClient()

    const { data: examData, error: examErr } = await supabase
      .from('exams')
      .select('*')
      .eq('id', examId)
      .single()

    if (examErr || !examData) {
      alert('Modul kegiatan tidak ditemukan!')
      router.push('/admin/practice')
      return
    }
    setExam(examData)

    const { data: questionData } = await supabase
      .from('questions')
      .select('*')
      .eq('exam_id', examId)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: true })

    if (questionData) setQuestions(questionData)
    setLoading(false)
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchExamAndQuestions()
  }, [examId])

  // 🟢 2. HANDLER TOMBOL 1: AUTO GENERATE SD (ACAK & APPEND)
  const handleAutoGenerateQuestions = async () => {
    const actType = exam?.activity_type || 'QUIZ'
    const subtestName = exam?.subtest || 'Matematika'

    const confirmGen = confirm(
      `Tambahkan soal otomatis dinamis ke "${exam.title}"?\n\n` +
      `Subtes: ${subtestName}\n` +
      `Bentuk Kegiatan: ${actType}\n` +
      `Tingkat: Sekolah Dasar (SD)\n\n` +
      `➕ Catatan: Soal variasi baru akan DITAMBAHKAN di bagian bawah (tanpa menghapus soal lama).`
    )
    if (!confirmGen) return

    const supabase = createClient()
    let generatedQuestions: any[] = []

    if (actType === 'QUIZ') {
      const lowerSub = subtestName.toLowerCase()

      if (lowerSub.includes('matematika') || lowerSub.includes('hitung') || lowerSub.includes('numerasi')) {
        const num1 = Math.floor(Math.random() * 20) + 10
        const num2 = Math.floor(Math.random() * 8) + 2
        const num3 = Math.floor(Math.random() * 50) + 10
        const ans1 = num1 * num2 + num3
        const side = Math.floor(Math.random() * 12) + 4
        const keliling = 4 * side

        generatedQuestions = [
          {
            exam_id: examId,
            question_text: `Berapakah hasil dari $${num1} \\times ${num2} + ${num3}$?`,
            options: [
              { key: 'A', text: `${ans1 - 10}` },
              { key: 'B', text: `${ans1}` },
              { key: 'C', text: `${ans1 + 10}` },
              { key: 'D', text: `${ans1 + 25}` },
            ],
            correct_option: 'B',
            explanation: `Perkalian didahulukan: $${num1} \\times ${num2} = ${num1 * num2}$, lalu $+ ${num3} = ${ans1}$.`,
          },
          {
            exam_id: examId,
            question_text: `Sebuah persegi memiliki panjang sisi $${side}\\text{ cm}$. Berapakah keliling persegi tersebut?`,
            options: [
              { key: 'A', text: `$${side * 2}\\text{ cm}$` },
              { key: 'B', text: `$${side * 3}\\text{ cm}$` },
              { key: 'C', text: `$${keliling}\\text{ cm}$` },
              { key: 'D', text: `$${side * side}\\text{ cm}$` },
            ],
            correct_option: 'C',
            explanation: `Keliling persegi = $4 \\times \\text{sisi} = 4 \\times ${side} = ${keliling}\\text{ cm}$.`,
          },
        ]
      } else if (lowerSub.includes('ipas') || lowerSub.includes('ipa') || lowerSub.includes('sains')) {
        generatedQuestions = [
          {
            exam_id: examId,
            question_text: 'Organ pernapasan utama pada manusia yang berfungsi menyerap oksigen adalah...',
            options: [{ key: 'A', text: 'Jantung' }, { key: 'B', text: 'Paru-paru' }, { key: 'C', text: 'Lambung' }, { key: 'D', text: 'Hati' }],
            correct_option: 'B',
            explanation: 'Paru-paru menyerap oksigen dan mengeluarkan karbondioksida.',
          },
          {
            exam_id: examId,
            question_text: 'Tumbuhan hijau membuat makanannya sendiri melalui proses yang disebut...',
            options: [{ key: 'A', text: 'Fotosintesis' }, { key: 'B', text: 'Respirasi' }, { key: 'C', text: 'Adaptasi' }, { key: 'D', text: 'Metamorfosis' }],
            correct_option: 'A',
            explanation: 'Fotosintesis memanfaatkan sinar matahari, air, dan CO2.',
          },
        ]
      } else if (lowerSub.includes('bahasa') || lowerSub.includes('indo') || lowerSub.includes('literasi')) {
        generatedQuestions = [
          {
            exam_id: examId,
            question_text: 'Gagasan utama yang menjadi inti pembahasan dalam sebuah paragraf disebut...',
            options: [{ key: 'A', text: 'Ide pokok' }, { key: 'B', text: 'Kalimat penjelas' }, { key: 'C', text: 'Kesimpulan' }, { key: 'D', text: 'Judul' }],
            correct_option: 'A',
            explanation: 'Ide pokok adalah pikiran utama dalam sebuah paragraf.',
          },
        ]
      } else if (lowerSub.includes('pancasila') || lowerSub.includes('pkn')) {
        generatedQuestions = [
          {
            exam_id: examId,
            question_text: 'Lambang Sila Pertama Pancasila "Ketuhanan Yang Maha Esa" adalah...',
            options: [{ key: 'A', text: 'Rantai Emas' }, { key: 'B', text: 'Bintang Emas' }, { key: 'C', text: 'Pohon Beringin' }, { key: 'D', text: 'Kepala Banteng' }],
            correct_option: 'B',
            explanation: 'Sila pertama dilambangkan Bintang Emas.',
          },
        ]
      } else if (lowerSub.includes('logis') || lowerSub.includes('logika') || lowerSub.includes('coding')) {
        const step = Math.floor(Math.random() * 3) + 2
        generatedQuestions = [
          {
            exam_id: examId,
            question_text: `Perhatikan deret pola berikut: $2, ${2 + step}, ${2 + step * 2}, ...$ Berapakah angka berikutnya?`,
            options: [
              { key: 'A', text: `${2 + step * 3 - 1}` },
              { key: 'B', text: `${2 + step * 3}` },
              { key: 'C', text: `${2 + step * 3 + 2}` },
              { key: 'D', text: `${2 + step * 4}` },
            ],
            correct_option: 'B',
            explanation: `Pola selalu bertambah $+${step}$.`,
          },
        ]
      } else {
        generatedQuestions = [
          {
            exam_id: examId,
            question_text: 'Warna primer yang tidak dapat dibuat dari campuran warna lain adalah...',
            options: [{ key: 'A', text: 'Hijau, Oranye' }, { key: 'B', text: 'Merah, Kuning, Biru' }, { key: 'C', text: 'Hitam, Putih' }, { key: 'D', text: 'Cokelat, Abu' }],
            correct_option: 'B',
            explanation: 'Warna primer terdiri dari Merah, Kuning, dan Biru.',
          },
        ]
      }
    } else if (actType === 'FLASHCARD') {
      generatedQuestions = [
        {
          exam_id: examId,
          question_text: `Kartu Memori Subtes ${subtestName} (#${questions.length + 1})`,
          options: [],
          correct_option: 'A',
          explanation: `Ringkasan konsep penting untuk ${subtestName}.`,
        },
      ]
    } else if (actType === 'MAZE_GAME') {
      const tx = Math.floor(Math.random() * 4)
      const ty = Math.floor(Math.random() * 4)
      generatedQuestions = [
        {
          exam_id: examId,
          question_text: `Misi Labirin SD #${questions.length + 1}: Pandu Robot ke Bintang di koordinat (${tx}, ${ty})!`,
          options: [],
          correct_option: 'FORWARD',
          target_x: tx,
          target_y: ty,
          explanation: 'Kombinasikan blok perintah Maju dan Belok.',
        },
      ]
    }

    const { error: insertErr } = await supabase.from('questions').insert(generatedQuestions)

    if (insertErr) {
      alert(`Gagal generate: ${insertErr.message}`)
    } else {
      alert(`🎉 Berhasil menambahkan ${generatedQuestions.length} soal variasi baru (${subtestName})!`)
      fetchExamAndQuestions()
    }
  }

  // 🟢 3. HANDLER TOMBOL 2: AUTO IMPORT PG DARI BANK SOAL UTAMA
  const handleImportFromBankSoal = async () => {
    const subtestName = exam?.subtest || 'Matematika'
    const confirmImport = confirm(
      `Impor soal PG Standar A-D yang sesuai dengan subtes "${subtestName}" dari Bank Soal lain?\n\n` +
      `➕ Catatan: Soal impor akan DITAMBAHKAN di bagian bawah (tanpa menghapus soal lama).`
    )
    if (!confirmImport) return

    setLoading(true)
    const supabase = createClient()

    const { data: allQuestions, error } = await supabase
      .from('questions')
      .select('*, exams(subtest, title)')
      .neq('exam_id', examId)
      .limit(150)

    if (error || !allQuestions || allQuestions.length === 0) {
      alert('Belum ada soal PG dari modul lain di Bank Soal Supabase.')
      setLoading(false)
      return
    }

    const currentSubtest = subtestName.trim().toLowerCase()

    let candidateQuestions = allQuestions.filter((q) => {
      const hasOptions = Array.isArray(q.options) && q.options.length === 4
      const correctOpt = String(q.correct_option || q.correct_answer || '').trim().toUpperCase()
      return hasOptions && ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'].includes(correctOpt)
    })

    if (candidateQuestions.length === 0) {
      alert('Tidak ditemukan soal PG Standar A-D dari modul lain.')
      setLoading(false)
      return
    }

    const matchedBySubtest = candidateQuestions.filter((q) => {
      const parentSubtest = (q.exams?.subtest || '').trim().toLowerCase()
      return parentSubtest && (parentSubtest.includes(currentSubtest) || currentSubtest.includes(parentSubtest))
    })

    const pool = matchedBySubtest.length > 0 ? matchedBySubtest : candidateQuestions
    const shuffled = [...pool].sort(() => 0.5 - Math.random())
    const selected = shuffled.slice(0, 5)

    const questionsToInsert = selected.map((q) => ({
      exam_id: examId,
      question_text: q.question_text,
      options: q.options,
      correct_option: String(q.correct_option || q.correct_answer || 'A').trim().toUpperCase(),
      explanation: q.explanation || q.pembahasan || '',
      image_url: q.image_url || null,
    }))

    const { error: insertErr } = await supabase.from('questions').insert(questionsToInsert)

    if (insertErr) {
      alert(`Gagal impor: ${insertErr.message}`)
    } else {
      alert(`✅ Berhasil mengimpor ${questionsToInsert.length} soal PG Standar (${subtestName}) ke bagian bawah!`)
      fetchExamAndQuestions()
    }
    setLoading(false)
  }

  // 🟢 4. HANDLER TOMBOL 3: IMPORT BULK (TEMPLATE MODAL ADAPTIF)
  const getBulkTemplateSample = () => {
    const actType = exam?.activity_type || 'QUIZ'
    if (actType === 'QUIZ') {
      return JSON.stringify(
        [
          {
            question_text: 'Berapakah hasil dari $15 \\times 3 + 20$?',
            options: [
              { key: 'A', text: '55' },
              { key: 'B', text: '65' },
              { key: 'C', text: '75' },
              { key: 'D', text: '85' },
            ],
            correct_option: 'B',
            explanation: 'Perkalian didahulukan: $15 \\times 3 = 45$, lalu $45 + 20 = 65$.',
          },
          {
            question_text: 'Organ tubuh manusia yang menyerap oksigen adalah...',
            options: [
              { key: 'A', text: 'Jantung' },
              { key: 'B', text: 'Paru-paru' },
              { key: 'C', text: 'Hati' },
              { key: 'D', text: 'Ginjal' },
            ],
            correct_option: 'B',
            explanation: 'Paru-paru adalah organ utama sistem pernapasan.',
          },
        ],
        null,
        2
      )
    } else if (actType === 'FLASHCARD') {
      return JSON.stringify(
        [
          {
            question_text: 'Berapakah Rumus Luas Segitiga?',
            explanation: 'Rumus: $L = \\frac{1}{2} \\times a \\times t$.',
          },
          {
            question_text: 'Berapakah Rumus Keliling Persegi?',
            explanation: 'Rumus: $K = 4 \\times s$.',
          },
        ],
        null,
        2
      )
    } else {
      return JSON.stringify(
        [
          {
            question_text: 'Misi Labirin #1: Arahkan Robot ke Bintang di titik (3, 2)!',
            target_x: 3,
            target_y: 2,
            explanation: 'Petunjuk: Gunakan perintah Maju 3x dan Belok Kanan.',
          },
        ],
        null,
        2
      )
    }
  }

  const handleOpenBulkModal = () => {
    setBulkInputText(getBulkTemplateSample())
    setIsBulkModalOpen(true)
  }

  const handleProcessBulkImport = async () => {
    if (!bulkInputText.trim()) {
      alert('Teks JSON impor massal tidak boleh kosong!')
      return
    }

    try {
      const parsedData = JSON.parse(bulkInputText)
      if (!Array.isArray(parsedData) || parsedData.length === 0) {
        alert('Format JSON harus berupa Array daftar soal [...]!')
        return
      }

      const actType = exam?.activity_type || 'QUIZ'
      const questionsToInsert = parsedData.map((item: any, idx: number) => {
        let payload: any = {
          exam_id: examId,
          question_text: item.question_text || `Item #${idx + 1}`,
          explanation: item.explanation || item.pembahasan || '',
          image_url: item.image_url || null,
        }

        if (actType === 'QUIZ') {
          payload.options = Array.isArray(item.options) ? item.options : []
          payload.correct_option = String(item.correct_option || item.correct_answer || 'A').toUpperCase()
        } else if (actType === 'MAZE_GAME') {
          payload.options = []
          payload.correct_option = 'FORWARD'
          payload.target_x = Number(item.target_x ?? 3)
          payload.target_y = Number(item.target_y ?? 3)
        } else if (actType === 'FLASHCARD') {
          payload.options = []
          payload.correct_option = 'A'
        }

        return payload
      })

      const supabase = createClient()
      const { error } = await supabase.from('questions').insert(questionsToInsert)

      if (error) {
        alert(`Gagal Impor Bulk: ${error.message}`)
      } else {
        alert(`🎉 Berhasil mengimpor ${questionsToInsert.length} item baru sekaligus!`)
        setIsBulkModalOpen(false)
        fetchExamAndQuestions()
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      alert(`⚠️ Format JSON tidak valid! Pastikan tanda petik dan koma sudah benar.\n\nDetail Error: ${err.message}`)
    }
  }

  // 🟢 5. HANDLER TOMBOL 4: TAMBAH / EDIT MANUAL
  const handleOpenModal = (q?: any) => {
    if (q) {
      setEditingQuestion(q)
      const opts = Array.isArray(q.options) ? q.options : []
      const getOptText = (key: string) => {
        const found = opts.find((o: any) => o.key === key || o.label === key)
        return typeof found === 'string' ? found : found?.text || ''
      }

      setFormData({
        question_text: q.question_text || '',
        option_a: getOptText('A') || (typeof opts[0] === 'string' ? opts[0] : opts[0]?.text || ''),
        option_b: getOptText('B') || (typeof opts[1] === 'string' ? opts[1] : opts[1]?.text || ''),
        option_c: getOptText('C') || (typeof opts[2] === 'string' ? opts[2] : opts[2]?.text || ''),
        option_d: getOptText('D') || (typeof opts[3] === 'string' ? opts[3] : opts[3]?.text || ''),
        correct_option: q.correct_option || q.correct_answer || 'A',
        explanation: q.explanation || q.pembahasan || '',
        image_url: q.image_url || '',
        target_x: q.target_x ?? 3,
        target_y: q.target_y ?? 3,
      })
    } else {
      setEditingQuestion(null)
      setFormData({
        question_text: '',
        option_a: '',
        option_b: '',
        option_c: '',
        option_d: '',
        correct_option: 'A',
        explanation: '',
        image_url: '',
        target_x: 3,
        target_y: 3,
      })
    }
    setIsModalOpen(true)
  }

  const handleSaveQuestion = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!formData.question_text.trim()) {
      alert('Isi pertanyaan / judul level wajib diisi!')
      return
    }

    const supabase = createClient()
    const actType = exam?.activity_type || 'QUIZ'

    let payload: any = {
      exam_id: examId,
      question_text: formData.question_text,
      explanation: formData.explanation,
      image_url: formData.image_url.trim() || null,
    }

    if (actType === 'QUIZ') {
      payload.options = [
        { key: 'A', text: formData.option_a },
        { key: 'B', text: formData.option_b },
        { key: 'C', text: formData.option_c },
        { key: 'D', text: formData.option_d },
      ]
      payload.correct_option = formData.correct_option
    } else if (actType === 'MAZE_GAME') {
      payload.options = []
      payload.correct_option = 'FORWARD'
      payload.target_x = Number(formData.target_x)
      payload.target_y = Number(formData.target_y)
    } else if (actType === 'FLASHCARD') {
      payload.options = []
      payload.correct_option = 'A'
    }

    if (editingQuestion) {
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('questions')
        .update(payload)
        .eq('id', editingQuestion.id)

      if (error) {
        alert(`Gagal memperbarui: ${error.message}`)
      } else {
        alert('✅ Data berhasil diperbarui!')
        setIsModalOpen(false)
        fetchExamAndQuestions()
      }
    } else {
      const { error } = await supabase.from('questions').insert([payload])

      if (error) {
        alert(`Gagal menyimpan: ${error.message}`)
      } else {
        alert('🎉 Item/Level baru berhasil ditambahkan!')
        setIsModalOpen(false)
        fetchExamAndQuestions()
      }
    }
  }

  const handleDeleteQuestion = async (qId: string) => {
    if (!confirm('Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus item ini?')) return
    const supabase = createClient()
    const { error } = await supabase.from('questions').delete().eq('id', qId)

    if (error) {
      alert(`Gagal menghapus: ${error.message}`)
    } else {
      setQuestions((prev) => prev.filter((q) => q.id !== qId))
    }
  }

  const actType = exam?.activity_type || 'QUIZ'

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-sans">
        <p className="font-semibold text-sm">Memuat Editor Aktivitas...</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-900 text-white font-sans select-none">
      {/* HEADER BAR DENGAN 4 TOMBOL AKSI */}
      <header className="bg-slate-950 border-b border-slate-800 px-6 py-4 flex flex-col lg:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
          <Link
            href="/admin/practice"
            className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl border border-slate-700 transition-colors"
          >
            ← Kembali ke Kelola Latihan
          </Link>
          <div>
            <span
              className={`text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-extrabold border uppercase ${
                actType === 'MAZE_GAME'
                  ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/40'
                  : actType === 'FLASHCARD'
                  ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/40'
                  : 'bg-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-300 border-indigo-500/40'
              }`}
            >
              {actType === 'MAZE_GAME'
                ? '🌀 LEVEL CODING MAZE'
                : actType === 'FLASHCARD'
                ? '🃏 KARTU FLASHCARD RUMUS'
                : '📝 BANK SOAL QUIZ (PG STANDAR)'}
            </span>
            <h1 className="text-lg font-black mt-0.5">
              {exam?.title} ({questions.length} {actType === 'QUIZ' ? 'Soal' : actType === 'FLASHCARD' ? 'Kartu' : 'Level'})
            </h1>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 4 TOMBOL AKSI */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {/* TOMBOL 1 */}
          <button
            onClick={handleAutoGenerateQuestions}
            className="px-3 py-2 bg-amber-500/20 hover:bg-amber-500/30 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/40 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl shadow-md transition-all flex items-center space-x-1"
            title="Tambah Soal Variasi SD di Bagian Bawah"
          >
            <span>⚡ Auto Generate SD</span>
          </button>

          {/* TOMBOL 2 */}
          {actType === 'QUIZ' && (
            <button
              onClick={handleImportFromBankSoal}
              className="px-3 py-2 bg-cyan-500/20 hover:bg-cyan-500/30 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/40 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl shadow-md transition-all flex items-center space-x-1"
              title="Import Soal PG Standar Sesuai Subtes"
            >
              <span>📥 Auto Import PG</span>
            </button>
          )}

          {/* TOMBOL 3 */}
          <button
            onClick={handleOpenBulkModal}
            className="px-3 py-2 bg-purple-500/20 hover:bg-purple-500/30 text-purple-300 border border-purple-500/40 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl shadow-md transition-all flex items-center space-x-1"
            title="Impor Massal Format JSON/Template"
          >
            <span>📦 Import Bulk</span>
          </button>

          {/* TOMBOL 4 */}
          <button
            onClick={() => handleOpenModal()}
            className="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-xl shadow-lg transition-all flex items-center space-x-1"
          >
            <span>➕ Tambah Manual</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main className="max-w-5xl mx-auto p-4 sm:p-6 space-y-6">
        {/* RINGKASAN MODUL */}
        <div className="bg-slate-950 p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 flex justify-between items-center text-xs">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <span className="text-slate-400 font-bold block">Detail Modul Kegiatan:</span>
            <p className="text-white font-bold">
              {exam?.title} — Kategori/Subtes: <span className="text-amber-400">{exam?.subtest || 'Matematika'}</span>
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="text-right font-mono">
            <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-indigo-600/20 text-indigo-300 border border-indigo-500/40 rounded-xl font-bold">
              Total {questions.length} Item Konten
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* DAFTAR ITEM/LEVEL TERSEDIA */}
        {questions.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {questions.map((q, idx) => {
              const correctOpt = String(q.correct_option ?? q.correct_answer ?? '').trim().toUpperCase()

              return (
                <div
                  key={q.id}
                  className="bg-slate-950 p-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 space-y-4 relative shadow-md"
                >
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-4 border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
                    <span className="font-extrabold text-indigo-400 text-xs font-mono">
                      {actType === 'QUIZ' ? `Soal Pilihan Ganda #${idx + 1}` : actType === 'FLASHCARD' ? `Kartu Memori #${idx + 1}` : `Misi Labirin #${idx + 1}`}
                    </span>
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleOpenModal(q)}
                        className="px-3 py-1 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl border border-slate-700 transition-colors"
                      >
                        ✏️ Edit
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleDeleteQuestion(q.id)}
                        className="px-3 py-1 bg-rose-500/20 hover:bg-rose-500/30 text-rose-300 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl border border-rose-500/40 transition-colors"
                      >
                        🗑️ Hapus
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="space-y-1">
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 uppercase font-bold block">
                      {actType === 'FLASHCARD' ? 'Kartu Sisi Depan (Pertanyaan/Rumus)' : 'Deskripsi Soal / Instruksi Misi:'}
                    </span>
                    <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-white leading-relaxed">
                      <MathRenderer content={q.question_text || ''} />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {q.image_url && (
                    <img src={q.image_url} alt="Gambar Soal" className="max-h-48 rounded-xl border border-slate-800" />
                  )}

                  {actType === 'QUIZ' && Array.isArray(q.options) && q.options.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-2">
                      {q.options.map((opt: any, oIdx: number) => {
                        const optKey = opt.key || String.fromCharCode(65 + oIdx)
                        const isCorrect = optKey === correctOpt

                        return (
                          <div
                            key={optKey || oIdx}
                            className={`p-3 rounded-xl border text-xs flex items-center space-x-2 ${
                              isCorrect
                                ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 border-emerald-500/50 text-emerald-200 font-bold'
                                : 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-800 text-slate-400'
                            }`}
                          >
                            <span className="w-5 h-5 rounded bg-slate-800 flex items-center justify-center font-mono text-[10px] shrink-0 font-bold">
                              {String.fromCharCode(65 + oIdx)}
                            </span>
                            <div className="flex-1">
                              <MathRenderer content={typeof opt === 'string' ? opt : (opt.text || opt.title || '')} />
                            </div>
                            {isCorrect && <span className="text-[10px] bg-emerald-500 text-slate-950 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-black">KUNCI</span>}
                          </div>
                        )
                      })}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {actType === 'FLASHCARD' && (
                    <div className="bg-amber-500/10 p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-amber-500/30 text-xs space-y-1">
                      <span className="font-bold text-amber-300 block">💡 Kartu Sisi Belakang (Pembahasan / Solusi):</span>
                      <div className="text-slate-200 leading-relaxed">
                        <MathRenderer content={q.explanation || q.pembahasan || 'Belum ada penjelasan.'} />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {actType === 'MAZE_GAME' && (
                    <div className="bg-emerald-500/10 p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/30 text-xs space-y-1">
                      <span className="font-bold text-emerald-300 block">🎯 Koordinat Bintang Target:</span>
                      <p className="font-mono text-white">X: {q.target_x ?? 3}, Y: {q.target_y ?? 3}</p>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {actType === 'QUIZ' && (q.explanation || q.pembahasan) && (
                    <div className="bg-slate-900/80 p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 text-xs space-y-1">
                      <span className="font-bold text-indigo-300 block">💡 Pembahasan / Solusi:</span>
                      <div className="text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
                        <MathRenderer content={q.explanation || q.pembahasan} />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="p-12 text-center text-slate-500 text-xs bg-slate-950 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 space-y-3">
            <p>Belum ada item/level yang tersimpan di dalam modul ini.</p>
            <div className="flex justify-center gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={handleAutoGenerateQuestions}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md"
              >
                ⚡ Auto Generate SD
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => handleOpenModal()}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md"
              >
                ➕ Tambah Item Pertama
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </main>

      {/* 📦 MODAL IMPORT BULK ADAPTIF */}
      {isBulkModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
          <div className="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 max-w-2xl w-full rounded-3xl p-6 space-y-4 text-white shadow-2xl animate-fadeIn max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
              <h2 className="text-sm font-bold flex items-center space-x-2">
                <span>📦</span>
                <span>Import Bulk (Impor Massal Modul: {actType})</span>
              </h2>
              <button onClick={() => setIsBulkModalOpen(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white font-bold text-sm">
                ✕
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-3">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-300 font-semibold">
                  Format JSON Template Sesuai Kegiatan (<span className="text-amber-400">{actType}</span>):
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setBulkInputText(getBulkTemplateSample())}
                  className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-purple-500/20 hover:bg-purple-500/30 text-purple-300 text-[11px] font-bold rounded-lg border border-purple-500/30"
                >
                  🔄 Muat ulang Template Sampel
                </button>
              </div>

              <textarea
                rows={12}
                value={bulkInputText}
                onChange={(e) => setBulkInputText(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-3 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-2xl text-xs font-mono text-emerald-300 focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-500 leading-relaxed"
                placeholder="Paste kode JSON array di sini..."
              />
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-end space-x-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-800">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsBulkModalOpen(false)}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition-colors"
              >
                Batal
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleProcessBulkImport}
                className="px-5 py-2 bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-500 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-xl shadow-md transition-all"
              >
                🚀 Proses Import Bulk
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ⚙️ MODAL FORM MANUAL */}
      {isModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
          <div className="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 max-w-xl w-full rounded-3xl p-6 space-y-4 text-white shadow-2xl animate-fadeIn max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
              <h2 className="text-sm font-bold flex items-center space-x-2">
                <span>⚙️</span>
                <span>
                  {editingQuestion ? 'Edit Data' : 'Tambah Baru'} (Mode:{' '}
                  {actType === 'MAZE_GAME' ? 'Coding Maze' : actType === 'FLASHCARD' ? 'Flashcard' : 'Quiz Standar'})
                </span>
              </h2>
              <button onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white font-bold text-sm">
                ✕
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSaveQuestion} className="space-y-4">
              {actType === 'QUIZ' && (
                <>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 mb-1">Teks Pertanyaan (Mendukung Formula KaTeX)</label>
                    <textarea
                      rows={3}
                      required
                      value={formData.question_text}
                      onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, question_text: e.target.value })}
                      placeholder="Contoh: Berapakah hasil dari $25 \times 4 + 50$?"
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="space-y-2 pt-1 border-t border-slate-800">
                    <span className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-300">Pilihan Jawaban (Tunggal A-D):</span>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                      <input type="text" value={formData.option_a} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, option_a: e.target.value })} placeholder="Opsi A" className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white" />
                      <input type="text" value={formData.option_b} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, option_b: e.target.value })} placeholder="Opsi B" className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white" />
                      <input type="text" value={formData.option_c} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, option_c: e.target.value })} placeholder="Opsi C" className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white" />
                      <input type="text" value={formData.option_d} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, option_d: e.target.value })} placeholder="Opsi D" className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white" />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 mb-1">Kunci Jawaban Benar (Pilihan Tunggal)</label>
                    <select value={formData.correct_option} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, correct_option: e.target.value })} className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white">
                      <option value="A">Pilihan A</option>
                      <option value="B">Pilihan B</option>
                      <option value="C">Pilihan C</option>
                      <option value="D">Pilihan D</option>
                    </select>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 mb-1">Pembahasan / Solusi</label>
                    <textarea rows={2} value={formData.explanation} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, explanation: e.target.value })} placeholder="Pembahasan langkah demi langkah..." className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white" />
                  </div>
                </>
              )}

              {actType === 'FLASHCARD' && (
                <>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 mb-1">Sisi Depan Kartu (Pertanyaan / Rumus)</label>
                    <textarea
                      rows={3}
                      required
                      value={formData.question_text}
                      onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, question_text: e.target.value })}
                      placeholder="Contoh: Berapakah Rumus Luas Lingkaran?"
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 mb-1">Sisi Belakang Kartu (Jawaban & Rumus Lengkap)</label>
                    <textarea
                      rows={3}
                      required
                      value={formData.explanation}
                      onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, explanation: e.target.value })}
                      placeholder="Contoh: $L = \pi \times r^2$ (dengan $\pi = 3.14$ atau $22/7$)."
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
                    />
                  </div>
                </>
              )}

              {actType === 'MAZE_GAME' && (
                <>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 mb-1">Instruksi Misi Labirin</label>
                    <textarea
                      rows={2}
                      required
                      value={formData.question_text}
                      onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, question_text: e.target.value })}
                      placeholder="Contoh: Bantu Robot Maskot mencapai Bintang di titik (3, 3) pada Papan Grid!"
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 mb-1">Koordinat Target X (0 - 3)</label>
                      <input type="number" min={0} max={3} value={formData.target_x} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, target_x: Number(e.target.value) })} className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white" />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 mb-1">Koordinat Target Y (0 - 3)</label>
                      <input type="number" min={0} max={3} value={formData.target_y} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, target_y: Number(e.target.value) })} className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white" />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 mb-1">Petunjuk Perintah (Hint)</label>
                    <textarea rows={2} value={formData.explanation} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, explanation: e.target.value })} placeholder="Gunakan perintah Maju 3x dan Belok Kanan..." className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white" />
                  </div>
                </>
              )}

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 mb-1">URL Gambar Tambahan (Opsional)</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={formData.image_url}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, image_url: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="https://..."
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-white"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-end space-x-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-800">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition-colors"
                >
                  Batal
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-5 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-xl shadow-md transition-all"
                >
                  💾 Simpan
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export default function BankSoalPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense
      fallback={
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-sans">
          <p className="font-semibold text-sm">Memuat Editor Aktivitas...</p>
        </div>
      }
    >
      <BankSoalContent />
    </Suspense>
  )
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: app/exam/[id]/page.tsx
====================================================================================================
'use client'

import { useEffect, useState, useRef, Suspense } from 'react'
import { useParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'
import MathRenderer from '@/components/MathRenderer'

function ExamEngineContent() {
  const params = useParams()
  const examId = params?.id as string
  const router = useRouter()

  const [exam, setExam] = useState<any>(null)
  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState<any[]>([])
  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState(0)

  // State ID Sesi & Status Real-Time (Ongoing / Paused)
  const [sessionId, setSessionId] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [sessionStatus, setSessionStatus] = useState<string>('ongoing')

  // State Jawaban Siswa & Ragu-ragu
  const [userAnswers, setUserAnswers] = useState<{ [key: string]: any }>({})
  const [flaggedQuestions, setFlaggedQuestions] = useState<{ [key: string]: boolean }>({})

  // 🎨 State Mode Tema (Light / Dark) & Ukuran Teks (Normal / Besar / Sangat Besar)
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<'light' | 'dark'>('light')
  const [fontSize, setFontSize] = useState<'normal' | 'large' | 'xlarge'>('normal')

  // State Modal Zoom Gambar (Lightbox)
  const [zoomedImageUrl, setZoomedImageUrl] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const [participantName, setParticipantName] = useState('')
  const [participantIdentifier, setParticipantIdentifier] = useState('')
  const [participantClass, setParticipantClass] = useState('')
  const [showQuestionNav, setShowQuestionNav] = useState(true)

  // Ref untuk menghindari stale closure dan submit ganda
  const userAnswersRef = useRef<{ [key: string]: any }>({})
  const questionsRef = useRef<any[]>([])
  const isSubmittingRef = useRef<boolean>(false)
  const sessionIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null)
  const flaggedQuestionsRef = useRef<{ [key: string]: boolean }>({})
  const autosaveTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null)
  const saveQueueRef = useRef<Promise<void>>(Promise.resolve())

  // Helper Acak Array (Fisher-Yates Shuffle)
  const shuffleArray = (array: any[]) => {
    const arr = [...array]
    for (let i = arr.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
      const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1))
      ;[arr[i], arr[j]] = [arr[j], arr[i]]
    }
    return arr
  }

  // 🟢 Helper parsing fleksibel data JSON string dari Supabase
  const parseJsonIfNeeded = (val: any) => {
    if (val === null || val === undefined) return val
    if (typeof val === 'string') {
      const trimmed = val.trim()
      if (
        (trimmed.startsWith('[') && trimmed.endsWith(']')) ||
        (trimmed.startsWith('{') && trimmed.endsWith('}'))
      ) {
        try {
          return JSON.parse(trimmed)
        } catch (e) {
          return val
        }
      }
    }
    return val
  }

  // Simpan seluruh jawaban dan status ragu-ragu ke Supabase secara berurutan.
  const persistAnswersToSupabase = async () => {
    const activeSessId = sessionIdRef.current
    const currentQuestions = questionsRef.current
    if (!activeSessId || currentQuestions.length === 0) return

    const answers = userAnswersRef.current
    const flags = flaggedQuestionsRef.current
    const updatedAt = new Date().toISOString()
    const records = currentQuestions.map((q) => {
      const raw = answers[q.id]
      const isObjectiveMultiple = q.question_type === 'complex_multiple_choice' || q.question_type === 'true_false_matrix'
      return {
        session_id: activeSessId,
        question_id: q.id,
        user_answer: raw === undefined || raw === null ? null : (typeof raw === 'string' ? raw : JSON.stringify(raw)),
        selected_option: q.question_type === 'multiple_choice' && typeof raw === 'string' ? raw : null,
        selected_answers: isObjectiveMultiple && raw !== undefined && raw !== null ? raw : null,
        is_flagged: Boolean(flags[q.id]),
        updated_at: updatedAt,
      }
    })

    // Jangan menulis ke sesi yang sudah berganti/dihapus.
    if (sessionIdRef.current !== activeSessId) return
    const { error } = await createClient().from('answers').upsert(records, {
      onConflict: 'session_id,question_id',
    })
    if (error) throw error
  }

  const queueAutosave = () => {
    saveQueueRef.current = saveQueueRef.current.then(() => persistAnswersToSupabase())
    return saveQueueRef.current
  }

  const scheduleAutosave = () => {
    if (autosaveTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(autosaveTimerRef.current)
    autosaveTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
      autosaveTimerRef.current = null
      queueAutosave().catch((error) => console.error('Autosave gagal:', error))
    }, 800)
  }

  const flushAutosave = async () => {
    if (autosaveTimerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(autosaveTimerRef.current)
      autosaveTimerRef.current = null
    }
    await queueAutosave()
  }

  // Update state, ref, cadangan lokal, lalu jadwalkan autosave 800 ms.
  const updateAnswersState = (newAnswersMap: { [key: string]: any }) => {
    setUserAnswers(newAnswersMap)
    userAnswersRef.current = newAnswersMap
    if (examId) localStorage.setItem(`cbt_answers_${examId}`, JSON.stringify(newAnswersMap))
    scheduleAutosave()
  }

  // 1. MEMUAT DATA UJIAN, BUKA/HUBUNGKAN SESI ONGOING SUPABASE, & HITUNG TIMER AKURAT
  const loadExamData = async () => {
    if (!examId) return
    
    const supabase = createClient()

    let user = null

    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.getUser()

      if (error) throw error

      user = data.user
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Gagal memeriksa login:', error)

      alert('Gagal memeriksa sesi login. Silakan coba kembali.')

      isSubmittingRef.current = false
      setIsSubmitting(false)
      return
    }

    if (!user) {
      alert('Sesi login tidak ditemukan. Silakan login kembali.')

      isSubmittingRef.current = false
      setIsSubmitting(false)
      router.push('/login')
      return
    }

    // Ambil identitas peserta dari tabel profiles berdasarkan ID akun login.
    const { data: participantProfile, error: profileError } = await supabase
      .from('profiles')
      .select('full_name, username, class_code')
      .eq('id', user.id)
      .maybeSingle()

    if (profileError) {
      console.error('Gagal mengambil profil peserta:', profileError)
    }

    setParticipantName(participantProfile?.full_name || 'Peserta Ujian')
    setParticipantIdentifier(participantProfile?.username || '')
    setParticipantClass(participantProfile?.class_code || '')

    // A. Ambil User Aktif
    const {
      data: { user: activeUser },
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser()

    if (!activeUser) {
      router.push('/login')
      return
    }

    // B. Fetch Data Ujian
    const { data: examData } = await supabase
      .from('exams')
      .select('*')
      .eq('id', examId)
      .single()

    if (!examData) {
      alert('Ujian tidak ditemukan!')
      router.push('/')
      return
    }

    // C. Fetch Data Soal
    const { data: questionData } = await supabase
      .from('questions')
      .select('*')
      .eq('exam_id', examId)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: true })

    if (questionData) {
      let processedQuestions = [...questionData]

      if (examData.shuffle_questions) {
        processedQuestions = shuffleArray(processedQuestions)
      }

      if (examData.shuffle_options) {
        processedQuestions = processedQuestions.map((q) => {
          if (
            (q.question_type === 'multiple_choice' ||
              q.question_type === 'complex_multiple_choice' ||
              q.question_type === 'true_false_matrix') &&
            Array.isArray(q.options) &&
            q.options.length > 0
          ) {
            return {
              ...q,
              options: shuffleArray(q.options),
            }
          }
          return q
        })
      }

      setQuestions(processedQuestions)
      questionsRef.current = processedQuestions
    }

    setExam(examData)

    // D. RESTORE JAWABAN DARI LOCALSTORAGE
    

    // E. 🟢 PROSES SESI REAL-TIME DI SUPABASE (AGAR MUNCUL DI LIVE PROCTORING)
    // E. Cari sesi terbaru, termasuk sesi yang sudah selesai
    let currentSessId: string | null = null
    let startTimeIso = new Date().toISOString()
    let currentSession: any = null

    
const { data: existingSession, error: sessionError } = await supabase
  .from('exam_sessions')
  .select('*')
  .eq('exam_id', examId)
  .eq('user_id', user.id)
  .in('status', ['ongoing', 'paused'])
  .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
  .limit(1)
  .maybeSingle()


    if (sessionError) {
      console.error('Gagal memuat sesi:', sessionError)
      alert('Gagal memuat sesi ujian. Silakan coba kembali.')
      setLoading(false)
      return
    }


    if (existingSession) {
      currentSession = existingSession
      currentSessId = existingSession.id
      startTimeIso = existingSession.start_time || startTimeIso
      setSessionStatus(existingSession.status)
    } else {
      // Sesi baru hanya dibuat saat tidak ada sesi yang tersisa.
      const { data: newSess, error: createError } = await supabase
        .from('exam_sessions')
        .insert({
          exam_id: examId,
          user_id: activeUser.id,
          status: 'ongoing',
          start_time: startTimeIso,
          paused_duration_seconds: 0,
          extra_time_seconds: 0,
        })
        .select('*')
        .single()

      if (createError || !newSess) {
        console.error('Gagal membuat sesi:', createError)

        alert(
          `Gagal membuat sesi ujian baru: ${
            createError?.message || 'Data sesi tidak dikembalikan oleh database.'
          }`
        )

        setLoading(false)
        return
      }

      currentSession = newSess
      currentSessId = newSess.id
      setSessionStatus('ongoing')
    }

    if (currentSessId) {
      setSessionId(currentSessId)
      sessionIdRef.current = currentSessId
    }

    // Buat placeholder untuk semua soal; ignoreDuplicates menjaga jawaban saat resume.
    if (currentSessId && questionsRef.current.length > 0) {
      const placeholders = questionsRef.current.map((q) => ({
        session_id: currentSessId,
        question_id: q.id,
        user_answer: null,
        selected_option: null,
        selected_answers: null,
        is_flagged: false,
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      }))
      const { error: placeholderError } = await supabase.from('answers').upsert(placeholders, {
        onConflict: 'session_id,question_id',
        ignoreDuplicates: true,
      })
      if (placeholderError) throw new Error(`Gagal membuat baris jawaban awal: ${placeholderError.message}`)
    }

    const localAnswersKey = `cbt_answers_${examId}`
    const localFlagsKey = `cbt_flags_${examId}`

    if (existingSession && currentSessId) {
      const { data: savedRows, error: savedRowsError } = await supabase
        .from('answers')
        .select('question_id, user_answer, selected_option, selected_answers, is_flagged')
        .eq('session_id', currentSessId)

      if (savedRowsError) console.error('Gagal memulihkan jawaban dari Supabase:', savedRowsError)

      const remoteAnswers: { [key: string]: any } = {}
      const remoteFlags: { [key: string]: boolean } = {}
      ;(savedRows || []).forEach((row: any) => {
        const q = questionsRef.current.find((item) => item.id === row.question_id)
        if (!q) return
        const type = q.question_type || 'multiple_choice'
        let value: any = row.user_answer
        if (type === 'multiple_choice') value = row.selected_option ?? row.user_answer
        else if (type === 'complex_multiple_choice' || type === 'true_false_matrix') {
          value = row.selected_answers ?? parseJsonIfNeeded(row.user_answer)
        }
        if (value !== null && value !== undefined) remoteAnswers[row.question_id] = parseJsonIfNeeded(value)
        remoteFlags[row.question_id] = Boolean(row.is_flagged)
      })

      let localAnswers: { [key: string]: any } = {}
      let localFlags: { [key: string]: boolean } = {}
      try {
        const savedAns = localStorage.getItem(localAnswersKey)
        if (savedAns) localAnswers = JSON.parse(savedAns) || {}
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Gagal memulihkan jawaban lokal:', e)
        localStorage.removeItem(localAnswersKey)
      }
      try {
        const savedFlags = localStorage.getItem(localFlagsKey)
        if (savedFlags) localFlags = JSON.parse(savedFlags) || {}
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Gagal memulihkan status ragu lokal:', e)
        localStorage.removeItem(localFlagsKey)
      }

      const mergedAnswers = { ...remoteAnswers, ...localAnswers }
      const mergedFlags = { ...remoteFlags, ...localFlags }
      setUserAnswers(mergedAnswers)
      userAnswersRef.current = mergedAnswers
      setFlaggedQuestions(mergedFlags)
      flaggedQuestionsRef.current = mergedFlags
    } else {
      localStorage.removeItem(localAnswersKey)
      localStorage.removeItem(localFlagsKey)
      localStorage.removeItem(`cbt_start_time_${examId}`)
      setUserAnswers({})
      userAnswersRef.current = {}
      setFlaggedQuestions({})
      flaggedQuestionsRef.current = {}
      setCurrentIndex(0)
    }

    // F. PERHITUNGAN TIMER SISA WAKTU BERBASIS TIMESTAMP START_TIME
    const durationSeconds =
      (Number(examData.duration_minutes) || 15) * 60

    const nowMs = Date.now()
    const startMs = new Date(startTimeIso).getTime()

    const pausedAtMs = currentSession?.paused_at
      ? new Date(currentSession.paused_at).getTime()
      : null

    const accumulatedPause =
      Number(currentSession?.paused_duration_seconds) || 0

    const extraTime =
      Number(currentSession?.extra_time_seconds) || 0

    const currentPauseSeconds = pausedAtMs
      ? Math.max(0, Math.floor((nowMs - pausedAtMs) / 1000))
      : 0

    const elapsedSeconds = Math.max(
      0,
      Math.floor((nowMs - startMs) / 1000) -
        accumulatedPause -
        currentPauseSeconds
    )

    const remainingSeconds = Math.max(
      0,
      durationSeconds + extraTime - elapsedSeconds
    )

    setTimeLeft(remainingSeconds)
    setLoading(false)
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadExamData()
  }, [examId])

  // 🟢 HOOK CEK STATUS SESI (PAUSE/RESUME) REAL-TIME
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!examId || loading) return

    const checkSessionStatus = async () => {
      const supabase = createClient()
      const {
        data: { user },
      } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
      if (!user) return

      
      const currentId = sessionIdRef.current

      if (!currentId) return

      const { data: activeSession, error } = await supabase
        .from('exam_sessions')
        .select('id, status')
        .eq('id', currentId)
        .eq('user_id', user.id)
        .maybeSingle()

      if (error) {
        console.error('Gagal memeriksa status sesi:', error)
        return
      }

      // Sesi telah dihapus oleh proktor melalui Reset Sesi.
      if (!activeSession) {
  // Hentikan proses submit lama agar tidak memakai sesi yang sudah dihapus.
  isSubmittingRef.current = false
  setIsSubmitting(false)

  // Hapus seluruh cadangan jawaban sesi sebelumnya.
  localStorage.removeItem(`cbt_answers_${examId}`)
  localStorage.removeItem(`cbt_flags_${examId}`)
  localStorage.removeItem(`cbt_start_time_${examId}`)
  if (autosaveTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(autosaveTimerRef.current)
  autosaveTimerRef.current = null
  flaggedQuestionsRef.current = {}

  // Reset state jawaban dan navigasi.
  setUserAnswers({})
  userAnswersRef.current = {}

  setFlaggedQuestions({})
  setCurrentIndex(0)

  // Kosongkan referensi sesi lama.
  sessionIdRef.current = null
  setSessionId(null)

  // Muat ulang untuk membuat sesi baru.
  window.location.replace(`/exam/${examId}`)
  return
}

      setSessionStatus(activeSession.status)

      // Sesi dipaksa selesai oleh proktor.
      if (activeSession.status === 'completed') {
        router.replace(
          `/exam/${examId}/summary?session_id=${activeSession.id}`
        )
      }

    }

    checkSessionStatus()
    const statusInterval = setInterval(checkSessionStatus, 3000)

    return () => clearInterval(statusInterval)
}, [examId, loading, sessionId, router])

  // 🟢 PROTEKSI RELOAD & BACK BROWSER
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleBeforeUnload = (e: BeforeUnloadEvent) => {
      if (!isSubmittingRef.current) {
        e.preventDefault()
        e.returnValue = ''
      }
    }
    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', handleBeforeUnload)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('beforeunload', handleBeforeUnload)
  }, [])

  // 🟢 TIMER COUNTDOWN PRESISI (HENTIKAN TIMER JIKA DIPAUSE)
      useEffect(() => {
        if (
      timeLeft === null ||
      timeLeft <= 0 ||
      sessionStatus === 'paused' ||
      sessionStatus === 'completed'
    ) {
      return
    }

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setTimeLeft((prev) => {
        if (prev !== null && prev <= 1) {
          clearInterval(timer)
          handleFinishExam(true)
          return 0
        }
        return prev !== null ? prev - 1 : null
      })
    }, 1000)

    return () => clearInterval(timer)
  }, [timeLeft, sessionStatus])

  const formatTime = (seconds: number) => {
    const mins = Math.floor(seconds / 60)
    const secs = seconds % 60
    return `${String(mins).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(secs).padStart(2, '0')}`
  }

  // Handler Jawaban Pilihan Ganda Biasa
  const handleSelectSingleOption = (questionId: string, optKey: string) => {
    const updated = { ...userAnswersRef.current, [questionId]: optKey }
    updateAnswersState(updated)
  }

  // Handler Jawaban PG Kompleks (Multiple Checkbox)
  const handleToggleComplexOption = (questionId: string, optKey: string) => {
    const currentAns = Array.isArray(userAnswersRef.current[questionId])
      ? userAnswersRef.current[questionId]
      : []
    let updatedList: string[] = []
    if (currentAns.includes(optKey)) {
      updatedList = currentAns.filter((k: string) => k !== optKey)
    } else {
      updatedList = [...currentAns, optKey]
    }
    const updated = { ...userAnswersRef.current, [questionId]: updatedList }
    updateAnswersState(updated)
  }

  // Handler Jawaban Matriks Benar-Salah
  const handleSelectMatrixValue = (
    questionId: string,
    statementId: string,
    val: 'Benar' | 'Salah'
  ) => {
    const currentMatrix =
      typeof userAnswersRef.current[questionId] === 'object' &&
      !Array.isArray(userAnswersRef.current[questionId])
        ? userAnswersRef.current[questionId]
        : {}
    const updatedMatrix = { ...currentMatrix, [statementId]: val }
    const updated = { ...userAnswersRef.current, [questionId]: updatedMatrix }
    updateAnswersState(updated)
  }

  // Handler Jawaban Essay
  const handleEssayChange = (questionId: string, text: string) => {
    const updated = { ...userAnswersRef.current, [questionId]: text }
    updateAnswersState(updated)
  }

  // Handler Ragu-Ragu
  const handleToggleFlag = (questionId: string) => {
    const updated = {
      ...flaggedQuestionsRef.current,
      [questionId]: !flaggedQuestionsRef.current[questionId],
    }
    flaggedQuestionsRef.current = updated
    setFlaggedQuestions(updated)
    if (examId) localStorage.setItem(`cbt_flags_${examId}`, JSON.stringify(updated))
    scheduleAutosave()
  }

  // 🟢 SELAYAKAN UJIAN, POPULASI NILAI PRESISI, & SIMPAN HASIL KE DATABASE
    const handleFinishExam = async (autoFinish = false) => {
    if (isSubmittingRef.current) return

    if (sessionStatus === 'completed') return

    if (!sessionIdRef.current) {
      alert('Sesi ujian tidak ditemukan. Silakan muat ulang halaman.')
      return
    }
    if (!autoFinish && !confirm('Apakah Anda yakin ingin menyelesaikan ujian ini?')) return

    isSubmittingRef.current = true
    setIsSubmitting(true)

    const supabase = createClient()

    let user: any = null

    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.getUser()

      if (error) throw error

      user = data.user
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Gagal memeriksa login:', error)

      alert('Gagal memeriksa sesi login. Silakan coba kembali.')

      isSubmittingRef.current = false
      setIsSubmitting(false)
      return
    }

    let totalPointsEarned = 0
    let totalMaxPoints = 0

    try {
      // Verifikasi sesi aktif sebelum flush; validasi penuh tetap dilakukan di bawah.
      await flushAutosave()
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Autosave terakhir gagal:', error)
      alert('Jawaban terakhir gagal disimpan. Ujian belum dikirim. Silakan coba lagi.')
      isSubmittingRef.current = false
      setIsSubmitting(false)
      return
    }

    const currentQuestions = questionsRef.current
    const currentAnswers = userAnswersRef.current
    const currentFlags = flaggedQuestionsRef.current

    currentQuestions.forEach((q) => {
      const qMaxScore = Number(q.max_score) || 1
      totalMaxPoints += qMaxScore

      const qType = q.question_type || 'multiple_choice'
      const userAns = currentAnswers[q.id]

      const isUnattempted =
        userAns === undefined ||
        userAns === null ||
        userAns === '' ||
        (Array.isArray(userAns) && userAns.length === 0) ||
        (typeof userAns === 'object' &&
          !Array.isArray(userAns) &&
          Object.keys(userAns).length === 0)

      if (!isUnattempted) {
        if (qType === 'multiple_choice') {
          const correctOpt = String(q.correct_option ?? q.correct_answer ?? '')
            .trim()
            .toUpperCase()
          if (String(userAns).trim().toUpperCase() === correctOpt) {
            totalPointsEarned += qMaxScore
          }
        } else if (qType === 'complex_multiple_choice') {
          const parsedCorrect = parseJsonIfNeeded(q.correct_answers)
          let correctKeys: string[] = []

          if (Array.isArray(parsedCorrect)) {
            correctKeys = parsedCorrect.map((k: any) => String(k).trim().toUpperCase())
          } else if (q.correct_option || q.correct_answer) {
            correctKeys = [String(q.correct_option || q.correct_answer).trim().toUpperCase()]
          }

          const userKeys: string[] = Array.isArray(userAns)
            ? userAns.map((k: any) => String(k).trim().toUpperCase())
            : []

          const correctUserChoices = userKeys.filter((k) => correctKeys.includes(k)).length
          const wrongUserChoices = userKeys.filter((k) => !correctKeys.includes(k)).length

          const netCorrect = Math.max(0, correctUserChoices - wrongUserChoices)
          if (correctKeys.length > 0) {
            const ratio = netCorrect / correctKeys.length
            totalPointsEarned += ratio * qMaxScore
          }
        } else if (qType === 'true_false_matrix') {
          const parsedCorrect = parseJsonIfNeeded(q.correct_answers)
          const correctObj: { [key: string]: string } =
            typeof parsedCorrect === 'object' && parsedCorrect !== null ? parsedCorrect : {}
          const userObj = typeof userAns === 'object' && !Array.isArray(userAns) ? userAns : {}

          const statementIds = Object.keys(correctObj)
          if (statementIds.length > 0) {
            let correctRows = 0
            statementIds.forEach((sId) => {
              if (
                userObj[sId] &&
                String(userObj[sId]).trim().toLowerCase() ===
                  String(correctObj[sId]).trim().toLowerCase()
              ) {
                correctRows++
              }
            })
            const ratio = correctRows / statementIds.length
            totalPointsEarned += ratio * qMaxScore
          }
        }
      }
    })

    const finalScore =
      totalMaxPoints > 0 ? Math.round((totalPointsEarned / totalMaxPoints) * 100 * 100) / 100 : 0

    
    const endTimeIso = new Date().toISOString()
    let activeSessId = sessionIdRef.current || sessionId

    try {
      // 1. Pastikan pengguna masih login
      if (!user) {
        throw new Error('Sesi login tidak ditemukan. Silakan login kembali.')
      }

      // 2. Pastikan sesi ujian tersedia
      // 2. Pastikan sesi yang akan diselesaikan masih ada dan aktif
      if (!activeSessId) {
        throw new Error(
          'Sesi ujian tidak ditemukan. Silakan muat ulang halaman.'
        )
      }

      const { data: activeSession, error: sessionCheckError } = await supabase
        .from('exam_sessions')
        .select('id, status')
        .eq('id', activeSessId)
        .eq('exam_id', examId)
        .eq('user_id', user.id)
        .maybeSingle()

      if (sessionCheckError) {
        throw new Error(
          `Gagal memeriksa sesi ujian: ${sessionCheckError.message}`
        )
      }

      if (!activeSession) {
        throw new Error(
          'Sesi ujian sudah dihapus atau tidak ditemukan. Muat ulang halaman untuk memulai kembali.'
        )
      }

      if (activeSession.status !== 'ongoing') {
        throw new Error(
          'Sesi ujian tidak lagi aktif. Muat ulang halaman untuk memeriksa status terbaru.'
        )
      }

      // 3. Siapkan semua jawaban, termasuk soal yang tidak dijawab
      const answerRecords = currentQuestions.map((q) => {
        const rawAns = currentAnswers[q.id]

        const formattedAns =
          rawAns === undefined || rawAns === null
            ? null
            : typeof rawAns === 'string'
              ? rawAns
              : JSON.stringify(rawAns)

        return {
          session_id: activeSessId,
          question_id: q.id,
          user_answer: formattedAns,
          selected_option:
            q.question_type === 'multiple_choice' &&
            typeof rawAns === 'string'
              ? rawAns
              : null,
          selected_answers:
            (q.question_type === 'complex_multiple_choice' ||
              q.question_type === 'true_false_matrix') &&
            rawAns !== undefined &&
            rawAns !== null
              ? rawAns
              : null,
          is_flagged: !!currentFlags[q.id],
          updated_at: endTimeIso,
        }
      })

      // 4. Simpan atau perbarui jawaban sebelum menyelesaikan sesi
      if (answerRecords.length > 0) {
        const { error: answersError } = await supabase
          .from('answers')
          .upsert(answerRecords, {
            onConflict: 'session_id,question_id',
          })

        if (answersError) {
          throw new Error(
            `Gagal menyimpan jawaban: ${answersError.message}`
          )
        }
      }

      // 5. Tandai sesi selesai hanya setelah penyimpanan jawaban berhasil
      const { data: completedSession, error: finishError } = await supabase
        .from('exam_sessions')
        .update({
          status: 'completed',
          score: finalScore,
          completed_at: endTimeIso,
          submitted_at: endTimeIso,
          end_time: endTimeIso,
        })
        .eq('id', activeSessId)
        .eq('user_id', user.id)
        .eq('status', 'ongoing')
        .select('id')
        .maybeSingle()

      if (finishError || !completedSession) {
        throw new Error(
          `Jawaban sudah dikirim, tetapi status selesai gagal diperbarui: ${
            finishError?.message || 'Sesi tidak ditemukan atau akses ditolak'
          }`
        )
      }

      // 6. Hapus cadangan lokal hanya setelah penyimpanan berhasil
      localStorage.removeItem(`cbt_answers_${examId}`)
      localStorage.removeItem(`cbt_flags_${examId}`)
      localStorage.removeItem(`cbt_start_time_${examId}`)

      router.push(
        `/exam/${examId}/summary?session_id=${activeSessId}`
      )
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Gagal menyelesaikan ujian:', error)

      const message =
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : 'Terjadi kesalahan yang tidak diketahui.'

      alert(
        `${message}\n\nJawaban lokal tidak dihapus. Silakan coba selesaikan ujian kembali atau hubungi proktor.`
      )

      isSubmittingRef.current = false
      setIsSubmitting(false)
