    }

  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-sans">
        <p className="font-semibold text-sm">Memuat ruang ujian CBT...</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const currentQuestion = questions[currentIndex]
  const qType = currentQuestion?.question_type || 'multiple_choice'

  const matrixStatements =
    Array.isArray(currentQuestion?.options) && currentQuestion.options.length > 0
      ? currentQuestion.options
      : Array.isArray(currentQuestion?.statements)
      ? currentQuestion.statements
      : Array.isArray(currentQuestion?.matrix_statements)
      ? currentQuestion.matrix_statements
      : []

  // 🎨 HELPER PEMETAAN TEMA & UKURAN TEKS DINAMIS
  const isDark = theme === 'dark'

  const textClass =
    fontSize === 'xlarge'
      ? 'text-lg leading-relaxed'
      : fontSize === 'large'
      ? 'text-base leading-relaxed'
      : 'text-sm leading-relaxed'

  return (
    <div
      className={`min-h-screen flex flex-col font-sans select-none transition-colors duration-200 ${
        isDark ? 'bg-slate-950 text-slate-100' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-900'
      }`}
    >
      {/* HEADER CBT SISWA */}
      <header className="bg-slate-900 text-white px-6 py-3 flex flex-wrap justify-between items-center shadow-md gap-3">
        <div>
          <span className="text-[10px] bg-indigo-500 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold uppercase">
            {exam?.subtest || 'TKA'}
          </span>
          <h1 className="text-base font-bold mt-0.5">{exam?.title}</h1>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center space-x-4 sm:space-x-6">
          {/* 🔠 CONTROLS FONT SIZE (UKURAN TEKS) */}
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-1 bg-slate-800 p-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-700 text-xs">
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold px-1 uppercase hidden sm:inline">
              Teks:
            </span>
            <button
              onClick={() => setFontSize('normal')}
              className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg font-bold text-[10px] transition-all ${
                fontSize === 'normal'
                  ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                  : 'text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700'
              }`}
              title="Ukuran Teks Normal"
            >
              A
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setFontSize('large')}
              className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg font-bold text-xs transition-all ${
                fontSize === 'large'
                  ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                  : 'text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700'
              }`}
              title="Ukuran Teks Besar"
            >
              A+
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setFontSize('xlarge')}
              className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm transition-all ${
                fontSize === 'xlarge'
                  ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                  : 'text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700'
              }`}
              title="Ukuran Teks Sangat Besar"
            >
              A++
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* 🌗 CONTROLS TEMA (DARK / LIGHT MODE) */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setTheme(isDark ? 'light' : 'dark')}
            className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl border border-slate-700 transition-colors flex items-center space-x-1"
            title="Ganti Tema Tampilan Ujian"
          >
            <span>{isDark ? '☀️ Mode Terang' : '🌙 Mode Gelap'}</span>
          </button>

          {/* TIMER */}
          <div className="text-right">
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block font-bold uppercase">
              Sisa Waktu
            </span>
            <span className="font-mono text-lg font-bold text-amber-400">
              ⏱ {timeLeft !== null ? formatTime(timeLeft) : '00:00'}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="hidden md:flex flex-col items-end max-w-[190px] leading-tight">
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-bold uppercase">Peserta</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-white font-semibold truncate max-w-full" title={participantName}>
              {participantName || 'Memuat identitas...'}
            </span>
            {participantIdentifier && (
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 truncate max-w-full" title={`Username: ${participantIdentifier}`}>
                @{participantIdentifier}
              </span>
            )}
            {participantClass && (
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 truncate max-w-full" title={`Kelas: ${participantClass}`}>
                Kelas {participantClass}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={() => setShowQuestionNav((visible) => !visible)}
            className="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl border border-slate-700 transition-colors"
            title={showQuestionNav ? 'Sembunyikan navigasi nomor soal' : 'Tampilkan navigasi nomor soal'}
            aria-label={showQuestionNav ? 'Sembunyikan navigasi nomor soal' : 'Tampilkan navigasi nomor soal'}
          >
            {showQuestionNav ? 'Sembunyikan Nomor' : 'Tampilkan Nomor'}
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => handleFinishExam(false)}
            disabled={isSubmitting || sessionStatus !== 'ongoing'}
            className="px-4 py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition-colors shadow-sm disabled:opacity-50"
            suppressHydrationWarning
          >
            {isSubmitting ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Selesaikan Ujian ✓'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* BODY KONTEN SOAL & NAVIGASI */}
      <div className={`flex-1 max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto p-6 grid grid-cols-1 ${showQuestionNav ? 'lg:grid-cols-4' : 'lg:grid-cols-1'} gap-6`}>
        {/* LEMBAR SOAL */}
        <div
          className={`${showQuestionNav ? 'lg:col-span-3' : 'lg:col-span-1'} p-6 rounded-2xl border shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between space-y-6 ${
            isDark ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-800' : 'bg-white border-slate-200'
          }`}
        >
          {currentQuestion ? (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div
                className={`flex justify-between items-center border-b pb-3 ${
                  isDark ? 'border-slate-800' : 'border-slate-200'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                  <span className={`font-bold ${isDark ? 'text-slate-100' : 'text-slate-800'} text-sm`}>
                    Soal Nomor {currentIndex + 1} dari {questions.length}
                  </span>
                  <span
                    className={`px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold rounded uppercase ${
                      isDark ? 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {qType === 'complex_multiple_choice'
                      ? 'PG Kompleks'
                      : qType === 'true_false_matrix'
                      ? 'Matriks'
                      : qType === 'essay'
                      ? 'Uraian / Essay'
                      : 'Pilihan Ganda'}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <button
                  onClick={() => handleToggleFlag(currentQuestion.id)}
                  disabled={isSubmitting || timeLeft === 0 || sessionStatus !== 'ongoing'}
                  className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-colors ${
                    flaggedQuestions[currentQuestion.id]
                      ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white'
                      : isDark
                      ? 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700'
                      : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200'
                  }`}
                  suppressHydrationWarning
                >
                  🚩 {flaggedQuestions[currentQuestion.id] ? 'Ragu-Ragu' : 'Tandai Ragu'}
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* TEKS SOAL & GAMBAR (DINAMIS SESUAI UKURAN FONT) */}
              <div
                className={`font-semibold ${textClass} ${
                  isDark ? 'text-slate-100' : 'text-slate-900'
                }`}
              >
                <MathRenderer content={currentQuestion.question_text || ''} />
              </div>

              {currentQuestion.image_url && (
                <div className="my-2">
                  <img
                    src={currentQuestion.image_url}
                    alt="Gambar Soal"
                    onClick={() => setZoomedImageUrl(currentQuestion.image_url)}
                    className="max-h-60 rounded-xl border cursor-zoom-in hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity border-slate-700"
                  />
                </div>
              )}

              {/* 1. PILIHAN GANDA BIASA */}
              {qType === 'multiple_choice' && Array.isArray(currentQuestion.options) && (
                <div className="space-y-2 pt-2">
                  {currentQuestion.options.map((opt: any, oIdx: number) => {
                    const optKey = opt.key || String.fromCharCode(65 + oIdx)
                    const displayLabel = String.fromCharCode(65 + oIdx)
                    const isSelected = userAnswers[currentQuestion.id] === optKey

                    return (
                      <button
                        key={optKey || oIdx}
                        onClick={() => handleSelectSingleOption(currentQuestion.id, optKey)}
                        disabled={isSubmitting || timeLeft === 0 || sessionStatus !== 'ongoing'}
                        className={`w-full text-left p-3.5 rounded-xl border flex items-center space-x-3 transition-all ${
                          isSelected
                            ? 'border-indigo-500 bg-indigo-950/70 ring-2 ring-indigo-500 text-indigo-200 font-bold'
                            : isDark
                            ? 'border-slate-800 bg-slate-800/80 hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 font-medium'
                            : 'border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-800 font-medium'
                        }`}
                        suppressHydrationWarning
                      >
                        <span
                          className={`w-6 h-6 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center font-mono font-bold text-xs shrink-0 ${
                            isSelected
                              ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                              : isDark
                              ? 'bg-slate-700 text-slate-200'
                              : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {displayLabel}
                        </span>
                        <div className={`flex-1 font-semibold ${textClass}`}>
                          <MathRenderer content={typeof opt === 'string' ? opt : (opt.text || opt.title || '')} />
                          {opt.image_url && (
                            <img
                              src={opt.image_url}
                              alt={`Opsi ${displayLabel}`}
                              onClick={(e) => {
                                e.stopPropagation()
                                setZoomedImageUrl(opt.image_url)
                              }}
                              className="max-h-24 mt-2 rounded border cursor-zoom-in hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity border-slate-700"
                            />
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </button>
                    )
                  })}
                </div>
              )}

              {/* 2. PILIHAN GANDA KOMPLEKS (CHECKBOX) */}
              {qType === 'complex_multiple_choice' && Array.isArray(currentQuestion.options) && (
                <div className="space-y-2 pt-2">
                  <p
                    className={`text-xs font-semibold p-2.5 rounded-xl border ${
                      isDark
                        ? 'bg-indigo-950/50 text-indigo-300 border-indigo-800'
                        : 'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-800 border-indigo-100'
                    }`}
                  >
                    ℹ️ Pilih satu atau beberapa jawaban yang Anda anggap benar.
                  </p>
                  {currentQuestion.options.map((opt: any, oIdx: number) => {
                    const optKey = opt.key || String.fromCharCode(65 + oIdx)
                    const displayLabel = String.fromCharCode(65 + oIdx)
                    const currentAns = Array.isArray(userAnswers[currentQuestion.id])
                      ? userAnswers[currentQuestion.id]
                      : []
                    const isSelected = currentAns.includes(optKey)

                    return (
                      <button
                        key={optKey || oIdx}
                        onClick={() => handleToggleComplexOption(currentQuestion.id, optKey)}
                        disabled={isSubmitting || timeLeft === 0 || sessionStatus !== 'ongoing'}
                        className={`w-full text-left p-3.5 rounded-xl border flex items-center space-x-3 transition-all ${
                          isSelected
                            ? 'border-indigo-500 bg-indigo-950/70 ring-2 ring-indigo-500 text-indigo-200 font-bold'
                            : isDark
                            ? 'border-slate-800 bg-slate-800/80 hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 font-medium'
                            : 'border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-800 font-medium'
                        }`}
                        suppressHydrationWarning
                      >
                        <input
                          type="checkbox"
                          readOnly
                          checked={isSelected}
                          className="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600 rounded"
                        />
                        <span className="font-bold text-xs shrink-0">{displayLabel}.</span>
                        <div className={`flex-1 font-semibold ${textClass}`}>
                          <MathRenderer content={typeof opt === 'string' ? opt : (opt.text || opt.title || '')} />
                          {opt.image_url && (
                            <img
                              src={opt.image_url}
                              alt={`Opsi ${displayLabel}`}
                              onClick={(e) => {
                                e.stopPropagation()
                                setZoomedImageUrl(opt.image_url)
                              }}
                              className="max-h-24 mt-2 rounded border cursor-zoom-in hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity border-slate-700"
                            />
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </button>
                    )
                  })}
                </div>
              )}

              {/* 3. MATRIKS BENAR - SALAH */}
              {qType === 'true_false_matrix' && (
                <div className="space-y-3 pt-2">
                  <p
                    className={`text-xs font-semibold p-2.5 rounded-xl border ${
                      isDark
                        ? 'bg-indigo-950/50 text-indigo-300 border-indigo-800'
                        : 'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-800 border-indigo-100'
                    }`}
                  >
                    ℹ️ Tentukan pilihan Benar atau Salah untuk setiap baris pernyataan di bawah ini.
                  </p>
                  <div
                    className={`border rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-xs ${
                      isDark ? 'border-slate-800 bg-slate-900' : 'border-slate-300 bg-white'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
                      <thead
                        className={`font-bold border-b ${
                          isDark
                            ? 'bg-slate-800/90 text-slate-100 border-slate-700'
                            : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-800 border-slate-300'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <tr>
                          <th className="p-3 font-extrabold text-xs">Pernyataan</th>
                          <th className="p-3 w-36 text-center border-l font-extrabold text-xs">
                            Pilihan Jawaban
                          </th>
                        </tr>
                      </thead>
                      <tbody className={`divide-y ${isDark ? 'divide-slate-800' : 'divide-slate-200'}`}>
                        {matrixStatements.map((st: any, sIdx: number) => {
                          const stId = String(st.id || sIdx + 1)
                          const currentMatrix =
                            typeof userAnswers[currentQuestion.id] === 'object'
                              ? userAnswers[currentQuestion.id]
                              : {}
                          const selectedVal = currentMatrix?.[stId]

                          const statementText =
                            typeof st === 'string'
                              ? st
                              : st?.text ||
                                st?.statement ||
                                st?.statement_text ||
                                st?.title ||
                                st?.content ||
                                st?.label ||
                                ''

                          return (
                            <tr
                              key={stId || sIdx}
                              className={`transition-colors ${isDark ? 'hover:bg-slate-800/50' : 'hover:bg-slate-50'}`}
                            >
                              <td className="p-3">
                                <div className="flex items-start space-x-2">
                                  <span className="font-bold text-xs shrink-0">{sIdx + 1}.</span>
                                  <div className={`flex-1 font-semibold ${textClass}`}>
                                    <MathRenderer content={statementText} />
                                    {st.image_url && (
                                      <img
                                        src={st.image_url}
                                        alt={`Gambar Pernyataan ${sIdx + 1}`}
                                        onClick={() => setZoomedImageUrl(st.image_url)}
                                        className="max-h-24 mt-1.5 rounded border cursor-zoom-in hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity border-slate-700"
                                      />
                                    )}
                                  </div>
                                </div>
                              </td>
                              <td className={`p-3 border-l ${isDark ? 'border-slate-800' : 'border-slate-200'}`}>
                                <div className="flex justify-center space-x-1.5">
                                  <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() =>
                                      handleSelectMatrixValue(currentQuestion.id, stId, 'Benar')
                                    }
                                    disabled={isSubmitting || timeLeft === 0 || sessionStatus !== 'ongoing'}
                                    className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg font-bold text-xs transition-all ${
                                      selectedVal === 'Benar'
                                        ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow-sm ring-2 ring-emerald-400'
                                        : isDark
                                        ? 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700'
                                        : 'bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700'
                                    }`}
                                    suppressHydrationWarning
                                  >
                                    Benar
                                  </button>
                                  <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() =>
                                      handleSelectMatrixValue(currentQuestion.id, stId, 'Salah')
                                    }
                                    disabled={isSubmitting || timeLeft === 0 || sessionStatus !== 'ongoing'}
                                    className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg font-bold text-xs transition-all ${
                                      selectedVal === 'Salah'
                                        ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white shadow-sm ring-2 ring-rose-400'
                                        : isDark
                                        ? 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700'
                                        : 'bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700'
                                    }`}
                                    suppressHydrationWarning
                                  >
                                    Salah
                                  </button>
                                </div>
                              </td>
                            </tr>
                          )
                        })}
                      </tbody>
                    </table>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* 4. URAIAN / ESSAY */}
              {qType === 'essay' && (
                <div className="space-y-2 pt-2">
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase">Lembar Jawaban Essay Anda:</label>
                  <textarea
                    rows={6}
                    placeholder="Tuliskan jawaban lengkap Anda di sini..."
                    value={userAnswers[currentQuestion.id] || ''}
                    disabled={isSubmitting || timeLeft === 0 || sessionStatus !== 'ongoing'}
                    onChange={(e) => handleEssayChange(currentQuestion.id, e.target.value)}
                    className={`w-full p-3.5 border rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-600 font-semibold shadow-inner ${textClass} ${
                      isDark
                        ? 'bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-500'
                        : 'bg-white border-slate-300 text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400'
                    }`}
                    suppressHydrationWarning
                  />
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-center py-10">Belum ada soal pada ujian ini.</p>
          )}

          {/* NAVIGASI SEBELUMNYA / SELANJUTNYA */}
          <div
            className={`flex justify-between items-center pt-4 border-t ${
              isDark ? 'border-slate-800' : 'border-slate-200'
            }`}
          >
            <button
              disabled={currentIndex === 0 || isSubmitting || timeLeft === 0 || sessionStatus !== 'ongoing'}
              onClick={() => setCurrentIndex((prev) => prev - 1)}
              className={`px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition-colors disabled:opacity-40 ${
                isDark ? 'bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200' : 'bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700'
              }`}
              suppressHydrationWarning
            >
              ← Sebelumnya
            </button>

            <button
              disabled={currentIndex === questions.length - 1 || isSubmitting || timeLeft === 0 || sessionStatus !== 'ongoing'}
              onClick={() => setCurrentIndex((prev) => prev + 1)}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
              suppressHydrationWarning
            >
              Selanjutnya →
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* SIDEBAR NAVIGASI NOMOR SOAL */}
        {showQuestionNav && <div
          className={`p-5 rounded-2xl border shadow-sm space-y-4 h-fit ${
            isDark ? 'bg-slate-900 border-slate-800' : 'bg-white border-slate-200'
          }`}
        >
          <h3
            className={`text-xs font-bold uppercase border-b pb-2 ${
              isDark ? 'border-slate-800 text-slate-300' : 'border-slate-200 text-slate-700'
            }`}
          >
            🧭 Navigasi Nomor Soal
          </h3>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-5 gap-2">
            {questions.map((q, idx) => {
              const ans = userAnswers[q.id]
              const isAnswered =
                ans !== undefined &&
                ans !== null &&
                (Array.isArray(ans)
                  ? ans.length > 0
                  : typeof ans === 'object'
                  ? Object.keys(ans).length > 0
                  : true)
              const isFlagged = !!flaggedQuestions[q.id]
              const isCurrent = idx === currentIndex

              let btnClass = isDark
                ? 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 border-slate-700'
                : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border-slate-200'

              if (isAnswered) btnClass = 'bg-emerald-600 text-white border-emerald-600 font-bold'
              if (isFlagged) btnClass = 'bg-amber-500 text-white border-amber-500 font-bold'
              if (isCurrent) btnClass += ' ring-2 ring-indigo-600 ring-offset-1 font-bold'

              return (
                <button
                  key={q.id}
                  onClick={() => setCurrentIndex(idx)}
                  disabled={isSubmitting || timeLeft === 0 || sessionStatus !== 'ongoing'}
                  className={`h-9 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono transition-all border flex items-center justify-center ${btnClass}`}
                  suppressHydrationWarning
                >
                  {idx + 1}
                </button>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        </div>}
      </div>

      {/* 🔒 OVERLAY LAYAR DIPAUSE / DIHENTIKAN SEMENTARA OLEH PENGAWAS */}
      {sessionStatus === 'paused' && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/90 backdrop-blur-md flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-white p-6 text-center font-sans">
          <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-amber-500/20 border-2 border-amber-400 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-4xl animate-bounce mb-4">
            ⏸️
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black">Ujian Dipause oleh Pengawas</h2>
          <p className="text-slate-300 text-xs max-w-md mt-2 leading-relaxed">
            Pengawas sedang menghentikan sementara aktivitas ujian Anda (misal untuk penanganan kendala teknis/jaringan).
            <br />
            <strong>Jawaban Anda telah tersimpan aman.</strong> Silakan tunggu hingga pengawas melanjutkan ujian.
          </p>
          <div className="mt-6 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-mono text-amber-300 animate-pulse">
            ⏳ Menunggu Pengawas Membuka Kuncian...
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODAL ZOOM GAMBAR (LIGHTBOX INTERAKTIF FULLSCREEN) */}
      {zoomedImageUrl && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4"
          onClick={() => setZoomedImageUrl(null)}
        >
          <div
            className="relative max-w-4xl max-h-[90vh] bg-white p-2 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col items-center justify-center"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setZoomedImageUrl(null)}
              className="absolute top-3 right-3 w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-slate-900/70 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm hover:bg-slate-900 transition-colors z-10"
              title="Tutup Gambar"
            >
              ✕
            </button>
            <img
              src={zoomedImageUrl}
              alt="Gambar Diperbesar"
              className="max-h-[85vh] max-w-full object-contain rounded-xl"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export default function ExamEnginePage() {
  return (
    <Suspense
      fallback={
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-sans">
          <p className="font-semibold text-sm">Memuat ruang ujian CBT...</p>
        </div>
      }
    >
      <ExamEngineContent />
    </Suspense>
  )
}



====================================================================================================
FILE: app/exam/[id]/summary/page.tsx
====================================================================================================
'use client'

import { useEffect, useState, Suspense } from 'react'
import { useParams, useSearchParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'
import { triggerVictoryConfetti } from '@/lib/confetti'
import { useTheme } from '@/context/ThemeContext'
import MathRenderer from '@/components/MathRenderer'
import Link from 'next/link'

// 🛠️ HELPER EVALUASI PRESISI UNTUK 4 TIPE SOAL (PG, PG KOMPLEKS, MATRIKS, ESSAY)

const evaluateQuestion = (q: any, userAns: any) => {
  const qType = q.question_type || 'multiple_choice'
  const qMaxScore = Number(q.max_score) || 1

  const normalize = (value: any) =>
    String(value ?? '').trim().toLowerCase()

  const isUnanswered =
    userAns === undefined ||
    userAns === null ||
    userAns === '' ||
    (Array.isArray(userAns) && userAns.length === 0) ||
    (
      typeof userAns === 'object' &&
      !Array.isArray(userAns) &&
      Object.keys(userAns).length === 0
    )

  if (isUnanswered) {
    return {
      isCorrect: false,
      isUnanswered: true,
      ratio: 0,
      points: 0,
    }
  }

  // 1. Pilihan Ganda Biasa
  if (qType === 'multiple_choice') {
    const correctOpt = normalize(
      q.correct_option ?? q.correct_answer ?? q.correct_key
    )
    const userOpt = normalize(userAns)
    const isCorrect = userOpt !== '' && userOpt === correctOpt

    return {
      isCorrect,
      isUnanswered: false,
      ratio: isCorrect ? 1 : 0,
      points: isCorrect ? qMaxScore : 0,
    }
  }

  // 2. PG Kompleks (Multiple Checkbox)
  if (qType === 'complex_multiple_choice') {
    const parseArray = (value: any): string[] => {
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        return value.map(normalize).filter(Boolean)
      }

      if (typeof value === 'string') {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(value)
          if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
            return parsed.map(normalize).filter(Boolean)
          }
        } catch {
          // Nilai bukan JSON; lanjutkan sebagai satu pilihan.
        }

        return value.trim() ? [normalize(value)] : []
      }

      return []
    }

    const correctKeys = [
      ...new Set(
        parseArray(
          q.correct_answers ??
          q.correct_option ??
          q.correct_answer
        )
      ),
    ]

    const userKeys = [
      ...new Set(parseArray(userAns)),
    ]

    if (correctKeys.length === 0) {
      return {
        isCorrect: false,
        isUnanswered: false,
        ratio: 0,
        points: 0,
      }
    }

    // Jawaban dinilai benar penuh hanya jika seluruh pilihan
    // sama persis dengan kunci, tanpa pilihan tambahan.
    const isCorrect =
      correctKeys.length === userKeys.length &&
      correctKeys.every((key) => userKeys.includes(key))

    // Skor parsial: pilihan benar dikurangi pilihan salah.
    const correctCount = userKeys.filter(
      (key) => correctKeys.includes(key)
    ).length

    const wrongCount = userKeys.filter(
      (key) => !correctKeys.includes(key)
    ).length

    const netCorrect = Math.max(0, correctCount - wrongCount)
    const ratio = Math.min(1, netCorrect / correctKeys.length)

    return {
      isCorrect,
      isUnanswered: false,
      ratio,
      points: ratio * qMaxScore,
    }
  }

  // 3. Matriks Benar - Salah
  
if (qType === 'true_false_matrix') {
  const correctObj =
    q.correct_answers &&
    typeof q.correct_answers === 'object' &&
    !Array.isArray(q.correct_answers)
      ? q.correct_answers
      : {}

  let userObj: Record<string, any> = {}

  if (
    typeof userAns === 'object' &&
    userAns !== null &&
    !Array.isArray(userAns)
  ) {
    userObj = userAns
  } else if (typeof userAns === 'string') {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(userAns)

      if (
        parsed &&
        typeof parsed === 'object' &&
        !Array.isArray(parsed)
      ) {
        userObj = parsed
      }
    } catch {
      userObj = {}
    }
  }

  const statementIds = Object.keys(correctObj)

  if (statementIds.length === 0) {
    return {
      isCorrect: false,
      isUnanswered: false,
      ratio: 0,
      points: 0,
    }
  }

  let correctRows = 0
  let answeredRows = 0

  for (const statementId of statementIds) {
    const correctValue = normalize(correctObj[statementId])
    const userValue = normalize(userObj[statementId])

    if (userValue !== '') {
      answeredRows++
    }

    if (
      userValue !== '' &&
      userValue === correctValue
    ) {
      correctRows++
    }
  }

  const ratio = correctRows / statementIds.length
  const isCorrect = correctRows === statementIds.length

  return {
    isCorrect,
    isUnanswered: answeredRows === 0,
    ratio,
    points: ratio * qMaxScore,
  }
}


  // 4. Essay / Uraian
  
  // Essay / Uraian - menunggu penilaian guru/admin
  if (qType === 'essay') {
    const essayText =
      typeof userAns === 'string'
        ? userAns.trim()
        : String(userAns ?? '').trim()

    const isFilled = essayText.length > 0

    return {
      isCorrect: false,
      isUnanswered: !isFilled,
      isPending: isFilled,
      ratio: 0,
      points: 0,
    }
  }

  return {
    isCorrect: false,
    isUnanswered: false,
    ratio: 0,
    points: 0,
  }
}

function ExamSummaryContent() {
  const params = useParams()
  const searchParams = useSearchParams()
  const router = useRouter()
  const { theme } = useTheme()

  const examId = params?.id as string
  const sessionId = searchParams.get('session_id')

  const [exam, setExam] = useState<any>(null)
  const [sessionData, setSessionData] = useState<any>(null)
  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState<any[]>([])
  const [userAnswersMap, setUserAnswersMap] = useState<{ [key: string]: any }>({})
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  // Filter pembahasan: 'all' | 'correct' | 'wrong'
  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState<'all' | 'correct' | 'wrong'>('all')
  const [zoomedImageUrl, setZoomedImageUrl] = useState<string | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    triggerVictoryConfetti()

    async function loadSummaryAndReview() {
      if (!examId) return
      const supabase = createClient()

      // 1. Data Ujian
      const { data: examData } = await supabase
        .from('exams')
        .select('*')
        .eq('id', examId)
        .single()

      if (examData) setExam(examData)

      // 2. Daftar Soal Ujian
      const { data: questionData } = await supabase
        .from('questions')
        .select('*')
        .eq('exam_id', examId)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: true })

      if (questionData) setQuestions(questionData)

      // 3. Data Sesi Ujian & Jawaban Siswa
      let currentSessionId = sessionId

      if (!currentSessionId) {
        const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
        if (user) {
          const { data: lastSession } = await supabase
            .from('exam_sessions')
            .select('*')
            .eq('exam_id', examId)
            .eq('user_id', user.id)
            .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
            .limit(1)
            .single()

          if (lastSession) {
            currentSessionId = lastSession.id
            setSessionData(lastSession)
          }
        }
      } else {
        const { data: session } = await supabase
          .from('exam_sessions')
          .select('*')
          .eq('id', currentSessionId)
          .single()

        if (session) setSessionData(session)
      }

      // 4. Jawaban Rinci dengan Auto-Parsing JSON
      if (currentSessionId) {
        const { data: answersData } = await supabase
          .from('answers')
          .select('*')
          .eq('session_id', currentSessionId)

        if (answersData) {
          const map: { [key: string]: any } = {}
          answersData.forEach((ans) => {
            let rawVal = ans.user_answer !== undefined && ans.user_answer !== null
              ? ans.user_answer
              : ans.selected_option

            if (typeof rawVal === 'string') {
              try {
                const parsed = JSON.parse(rawVal)
                if (parsed !== null) rawVal = parsed
              } catch {
                // Biarkan string biasa
              }
            }
            map[ans.question_id] = rawVal
          })
          setUserAnswersMap(map)
        }
      }

      setLoading(false)
    }

    loadSummaryAndReview()
  }, [examId, sessionId])

  // Hitung Skor & Statistik
  let totalPointsEarned = 0
  let totalMaxPoints = 0
  let totalCorrect = 0
  let totalWrong = 0
  let totalUnanswered = 0

  questions.forEach((q) => {
    const qMaxScore = Number(q.max_score) || 1
    totalMaxPoints += qMaxScore

    const userAns = userAnswersMap[q.id]
    const evalResult = evaluateQuestion(q, userAns)

    if (evalResult.isUnanswered) {
      totalUnanswered++
    } else if (evalResult.isCorrect) {
      totalCorrect++
      totalPointsEarned += evalResult.points
    } else {
      totalWrong++
      totalPointsEarned += evalResult.points
    }
  })

  const calculatedScore = totalMaxPoints > 0
    ? Math.round((totalPointsEarned / totalMaxPoints) * 100)
    : 0

  // Auto Update database jika nilai / end_time null
  

  // Filter Soal
  const filteredQuestions = questions.filter((q) => {
    const userAns = userAnswersMap[q.id]
    const evalResult = evaluateQuestion(q, userAns)

    if (filterType === 'correct') return evalResult.isCorrect
    if (filterType === 'wrong') return !evalResult.isCorrect
    return true
  })

  // 🎨 Theme Styles (Dukungan Lengkap 6 Tema & Safe Fallback)
  const themeStylesMap: Record<string, { card: string; statBg: string; badgeColor: string }> = {
    space: {
      card: 'bg-slate-900/85 border-purple-500/40 text-white shadow-purple-900/40',
      statBg: 'bg-purple-950/60 border-purple-800/50',
      badgeColor: 'bg-purple-900/80 text-purple-200 border-purple-700/50',
    },
    jungle: {
      card: 'bg-emerald-950/85 border-emerald-500/40 text-white shadow-emerald-950/40',
      statBg: 'bg-emerald-900/60 border-emerald-800/50',
      badgeColor: 'bg-emerald-900/80 text-emerald-200 border-emerald-700/50',
    },
    ocean: {
      card: 'bg-sky-950/85 border-cyan-500/40 text-white shadow-sky-950/40',
      statBg: 'bg-sky-900/60 border-cyan-800/50',
      badgeColor: 'bg-sky-900/80 text-cyan-200 border-cyan-700/50',
    },
    disney: {
      card: 'bg-purple-950/90 border-2 border-amber-400/40 text-white shadow-pink-950/50',
      statBg: 'bg-purple-900/60 border-amber-400/40 text-amber-200',
      badgeColor: 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border-amber-400/50',
    },
    cartoon3d: {
      card: 'bg-white border-4 border-slate-900 text-slate-900 shadow-[5px_5px_0px_0px_rgba(15,23,42,1)]',
      statBg: 'bg-amber-100 border-2 border-slate-900 text-slate-900',
      badgeColor: 'bg-yellow-200 text-slate-900 border-2 border-slate-900 font-black',
    },
    school: {
      card: 'bg-white/95 border-slate-200 text-slate-800 shadow-slate-200/50',
      statBg: 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200',
      badgeColor: 'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 border-indigo-200',
    },
  }

  const themeStyles = themeStylesMap[theme] || themeStylesMap.school

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center font-sans">
        <div className="p-6 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900/70 text-white backdrop-blur-md border border-white/10 text-sm font-semibold">
          Memuat ringkasan & pembahasan nilai... ⏳
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen p-4 sm:p-8 font-sans relative z-10 max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-8">
      
      {/* 1. KARTU UTAMA SKOR & PRESTASI */}
      <div className={`p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-3xl border backdrop-blur-2xl shadow-2xl space-y-6 transition-all ${themeStyles.card}`}>
        
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4 text-center sm:text-left border-b border-white/10 pb-6">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <span className={`inline-block px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-extrabold border uppercase tracking-wider ${themeStyles.badgeColor}`}>
              🏆 HASIL UJIAN SELESAI
            </span>
            <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-black tracking-tight">
              {exam?.title || 'Evaluasi Ujian TKA'}
            </h1>
            <p className="text-xs opacity-80">
              Subtes: <strong className="uppercase">{exam?.subtest || 'Umum'}</strong>
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 bg-white/10 px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl border border-white/15">
            <div className="text-4xl animate-bounce">⭐</div>
            <div className="text-right">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase opacity-75 block">Skor Akhir</span>
              <span className="text-3xl font-black text-amber-400">
                  {sessionData?.score !== null && sessionData?.score !== undefined
                    ? Number(sessionData.score)
                    : calculatedScore} / 100
                </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* STATISTIK RINGKAS */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3 text-center">
          <div className={`p-4 rounded-2xl border backdrop-blur-md ${themeStyles.statBg}`}>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase opacity-75 block">Total Soal</span>
            <span className="text-xl font-black">{questions.length}</span>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl border bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-400">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase opacity-75 block">Jawaban Benar</span>
            <span className="text-xl font-black">🟢 {totalCorrect}</span>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl border bg-rose-500/10 border-rose-500/30 text-rose-400">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase opacity-75 block">Salah / Kosong</span>
            <span className="text-xl font-black">🔴 {totalWrong + totalUnanswered}</span>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl border bg-sky-500/10 border-sky-500/30 text-sky-400">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase opacity-75 block">Akurasi Nilai</span>
            <span className="text-xl font-black">🎯 {calculatedScore}%</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* NAVIGASI CEPAT */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-between items-center gap-3 pt-2">
          <Link
            href="/"
            className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-blue-700 hover:to-indigo-700 text-white font-extrabold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-lg transition-all active:scale-95"
          >
            🏠 Kembali ke Beranda Ujian
          </Link>

          <Link
            href="/history"
            className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl border border-white/20 transition-all"
          >
            📊 Lihat Semua Riwayat Nilai
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 2. BAGIAN PEMBAHASAN SOAL */}
      <div className={`p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-3xl border backdrop-blur-2xl shadow-2xl space-y-6 ${themeStyles.card}`}>
        
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4 border-b border-white/10 pb-4">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-black flex items-center space-x-2">
              <span>📖</span>
              <span>Pembahasan & Kunci Jawaban</span>
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs opacity-75 mt-0.5">
              Pelajari kembali soal dan kunci jawaban resmi untuk meningkatkan pemahaman.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* FILTER TAB */}
          <div className="flex bg-black/20 p-1 rounded-xl border border-white/10 space-x-1 text-xs font-bold">
            <button
              onClick={() => setFilterType('all')}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                filterType === 'all' ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow' : 'opacity-70 hover:opacity-100'
              }`}
            >
              Semua ({questions.length})
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setFilterType('correct')}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                filterType === 'correct' ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow' : 'opacity-70 hover:opacity-100'
              }`}
            >
              Benar ({totalCorrect})
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setFilterType('wrong')}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                filterType === 'wrong' ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white shadow' : 'opacity-70 hover:opacity-100'
              }`}
            >
              Salah ({totalWrong + totalUnanswered})
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* DAFTAR SOAL & PEMBAHASAN */}
        <div className="space-y-6">
          {filteredQuestions.length > 0 ? (
            filteredQuestions.map((q) => {
              const userAns = userAnswersMap[q.id]
              const evalResult = evaluateQuestion(q, userAns)
              const qType = q.question_type || 'multiple_choice'

              return (
                <div
                  key={q.id}
                  className={`p-5 rounded-2xl border transition-all space-y-4 ${
                    evalResult.isCorrect
                      ? 'bg-emerald-950/20 border-emerald-500/30'
                      : 'bg-rose-950/20 border-rose-500/30'
                  }`}
                >
                  {/* HEAD SOAL */}
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-b border-white/10 pb-3">
                    <span className="font-extrabold text-xs sm:text-sm flex items-center space-x-2">
                      <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 bg-white/10 rounded-lg border border-white/10">
                        Soal #{questions.findIndex((item) => item.id === q.id) + 1}
                      </span>
                      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 bg-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-300 text-[10px] font-bold rounded uppercase border border-indigo-500/30">
                        {qType === 'complex_multiple_choice'
                          ? 'PG Kompleks'
                          : qType === 'true_false_matrix'
                          ? 'Matriks'
                          : qType === 'essay'
                          ? 'Essay'
                          : 'Pilihan Ganda'}
                      </span>
                    </span>

                    {/* BADGE STATUS */}
                    {evalResult.isCorrect ? (
                      <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/40 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl flex items-center space-x-1">
                        <span>✅</span>
                        <span>Jawaban Benar</span>
                      </span>
                    ) : evalResult.isUnanswered ? (
                      <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/40 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl flex items-center space-x-1">
                        <span>⚠️</span>
                        <span>Tidak Dijawab</span>
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="px-3 py-1 bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/40 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl flex items-center space-x-1">
                        <span>❌</span>
                        <span>Jawaban Kurang Tepat</span>
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* TEKS SOAL */}
                  <div className="text-sm font-medium leading-relaxed">
                    <MathRenderer content={q.question_text} />
                  </div>

                  {/* GAMBAR SOAL JIKA ADA */}
                  {q.image_url && (
                    <div className="my-2">
                      <img
                        src={q.image_url}
                        alt="Gambar Soal"
                        className="max-h-56 rounded-xl border border-white/20 cursor-pointer object-contain hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity"
                        onClick={() => setZoomedImageUrl(q.image_url)}
                      />
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* RENDER HASIL SESUAI TIPE SOAL */}

                  {/* 1. PILIHAN GANDA BIASA */}
                  {qType === 'multiple_choice' && Array.isArray(q.options) && (
                    <div className="space-y-2 pt-1">
                      {q.options.map((opt: any, oIdx: number) => {
                        const optKey = opt.key || String.fromCharCode(65 + oIdx)
                        const correctOpt = String(q.correct_option ?? q.correct_answer ?? q.correct_key ?? '').trim().toUpperCase()
                        const userOpt = String(userAns || '').trim().toUpperCase()

                        const isSelected = userOpt === optKey.toUpperCase()
                        const isCorrectOpt = correctOpt === optKey.toUpperCase()

                        let optionStyle = 'border-white/10 bg-white/5 opacity-80'
                        if (isCorrectOpt) {
                          optionStyle = 'border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-200 font-bold ring-1 ring-emerald-400'
                        } else if (isSelected && !evalResult.isCorrect) {
                          optionStyle = 'border-rose-500 bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-200 font-bold ring-1 ring-rose-400'
                        }

                        return (
                          <div
                            key={optKey}
                            className={`p-3 rounded-xl border text-xs flex items-center justify-between transition-all ${optionStyle}`}
                          >
                            <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
                              <span
                                className={`w-6 h-6 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center font-mono font-bold text-xs shrink-0 ${
                                  isCorrectOpt
                                    ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-white'
                                    : isSelected
                                    ? 'bg-rose-500 text-white'
                                    : 'bg-white/10 text-white/80'
                                }`}
                              >
                                {optKey}
                              </span>
                              <div>
                                <MathRenderer content={typeof opt === 'string' ? opt : opt.text} />
                              </div>
                            </div>

                            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-[10px] font-bold">
                              {isSelected && <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-white/20">Jawabanmu</span>}
                              {isCorrectOpt && (
                                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-400/40">
                                  Kunci Jawaban ✔
                                </span>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        )
                      })}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* 2. PG KOMPLEKS */}
                  {qType === 'complex_multiple_choice' && Array.isArray(q.options) && (
                    <div className="space-y-2 pt-1">
                      {q.options.map((opt: any, oIdx: number) => {
                        const optKey = opt.key || String.fromCharCode(65 + oIdx)
                        const correctKeys: string[] = Array.isArray(q.correct_answers)
                          ? q.correct_answers.map((k: any) => String(k).trim().toUpperCase())
                          : [String(q.correct_option || q.correct_answer || '').trim().toUpperCase()]

                        let userKeys: string[] = []
                        if (Array.isArray(userAns)) {
                          userKeys = userAns.map((k: any) => String(k).trim().toUpperCase())
                        } else if (typeof userAns === 'string') {
                          try {
                            const parsed = JSON.parse(userAns)
                            if (Array.isArray(parsed)) userKeys = parsed.map((k: any) => String(k).trim().toUpperCase())
                            else userKeys = [userAns.trim().toUpperCase()]
                          } catch {
                            userKeys = [userAns.trim().toUpperCase()]
                          }
                        }

                        const isSelected = userKeys.includes(optKey.toUpperCase())
                        const isCorrectOpt = correctKeys.includes(optKey.toUpperCase())

                        let optionStyle = 'border-white/10 bg-white/5 opacity-80'
                        if (isCorrectOpt) {
                          optionStyle = 'border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-200 font-bold ring-1 ring-emerald-400'
                        } else if (isSelected && !isCorrectOpt) {
                          optionStyle = 'border-rose-500 bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-200 font-bold ring-1 ring-rose-400'
                        }

                        return (
                          <div
                            key={optKey}
                            className={`p-3 rounded-xl border text-xs flex items-center justify-between transition-all ${optionStyle}`}
                          >
                            <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
                              <input type="checkbox" readOnly checked={isSelected} className="rounded" />
                              <span className="font-bold">{optKey}.</span>
                              <div>
                                <MathRenderer content={typeof opt === 'string' ? opt : opt.text} />
                              </div>
                            </div>

                            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-[10px] font-bold">
                              {isSelected && <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-white/20">Jawabanmu</span>}
                              {isCorrectOpt && (
                                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-400/40">
                                  Kunci Jawaban ✔
                                </span>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        )
                      })}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* 3. MATRIKS BENAR - SALAH */}
                  {qType === 'true_false_matrix' && Array.isArray(q.options) && (
                    <div className="border border-white/10 rounded-xl overflow-hidden text-xs">
                      <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
                        <thead className="bg-white/10 font-bold text-white/90 border-b border-white/10">
                          <tr>
                            <th className="p-3">Pernyataan</th>
                            <th className="p-3 w-28 text-center border-l border-white/10">Jawabanmu</th>
                            <th className="p-3 w-28 text-center border-l border-white/10">Kunci Jawaban</th>
                          </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody className="divide-y divide-white/10">
                          {q.options.map((st: any, sIdx: number) => {
                            const stId = String(st.id || sIdx + 1)
                            const correctObj = q.correct_answers || {}

                            let userObj: { [key: string]: string } = {}
                            if (typeof userAns === 'object' && userAns !== null && !Array.isArray(userAns)) {
                              userObj = userAns
                            } else if (typeof userAns === 'string') {
                              try {
                                userObj = JSON.parse(userAns)
                              } catch {
                                userObj = {}
                              }
                            }

                            const userVal = userObj[stId] || 'Kosong'
                            const correctVal = correctObj[stId] || 'Benar'
                            const isRowCorrect = String(userVal).trim().toLowerCase() === String(correctVal).trim().toLowerCase()

                            return (
                              <tr key={stId} className="hover:bg-white/5">
                                <td className="p-3">
                                  <MathRenderer content={typeof st === 'string' ? st : st.text} />
                                </td>
                                <td className="p-3 text-center border-l border-white/10 font-bold">
                                  <span
                                    className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded ${
                                      isRowCorrect
                                        ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300'
                                        : 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300'
                                    }`}
                                  >
                                    {userVal}
                                  </span>
                                </td>
                                <td className="p-3 text-center border-l border-white/10 font-bold text-emerald-300">
                                  {correctVal}
                                </td>
                              </tr>
                            )
                          })}
                        </tbody>
                      </table>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* 4. ESSAY */}
                  {qType === 'essay' && (
                    <div className="p-3 bg-white/5 border border-white/10 rounded-xl text-xs space-y-1">
                      <span className="font-bold text-amber-300">Jawaban Essay Siswa:</span>
                      <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-white/90">{typeof userAns === 'string' ? userAns : '(Belum dijawab)'}</p>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* PEMBAHASAN SOAL */}
                  {(q.explanation || q.pembahasan) && (
                    <div className="mt-3 p-4 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30 text-amber-200 text-xs space-y-1">
                      <span className="font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider block flex items-center space-x-1">
                        <span>💡</span>
                        <span>Pembahasan Soal:</span>
                      </span>
                      <div className="leading-relaxed opacity-95">
                        <MathRenderer content={q.explanation || q.pembahasan} />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}

                </div>
              )
            })
          ) : (
            <div className="p-8 text-center text-xs opacity-75 bg-white/5 rounded-2xl border border-white/10">
              Tidak ada soal pada kategori filter ini.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* MODAL ZOOM GAMBAR SOAL */}
      {zoomedImageUrl && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/90 backdrop-blur-md z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 cursor-zoom-out"
          onClick={() => setZoomedImageUrl(null)}
        >
          <div className="relative max-w-4xl max-h-[90vh] bg-slate-900 p-3 rounded-2xl border border-white/20 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col items-center">
            <button
              onClick={() => setZoomedImageUrl(null)}
              className="self-end px-3 py-1 bg-rose-600 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-lg mb-2"
            >
              ✕ Tutup
            </button>
            <img
              src={zoomedImageUrl}
              alt="Gambar Diperbesar"
              className="max-w-full max-h-[80vh] object-contain rounded-lg"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  )
}

export default function ExamSummaryPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense
      fallback={
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center font-sans">
          <div className="p-6 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900/70 text-white backdrop-blur-md border border-white/10 text-sm font-semibold">
            Memuat ringkasan & pembahasan nilai... ⏳
          </div>
        </div>
      }
    >
      <ExamSummaryContent />
    </Suspense>
  )
}
