'use client'

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, Suspense } from 'react'
import { useParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'
import MathRenderer from '@/components/MathRenderer'
import Link from 'next/link'
import MazeGameEngine from '@/components/games/MazeGameEngine'
import FlashcardEngine from '@/components/games/FlashcardEngine'

function normalizeKey(value: any): string {
  return String(value ?? '').trim().toUpperCase()
}

function normalizeTruth(value: any): string | null {
  const normalized = String(value ?? '').trim().toLowerCase()

  if (['benar', 'true', '1', 'b'].includes(normalized)) return 'benar'
  if (['salah', 'false', '0', 's'].includes(normalized)) return 'salah'

  return null
}

function parseComplexAnswer(value: any): string[] {
  let parsed = value

  if (typeof parsed === 'string') {
    try {
      parsed = JSON.parse(parsed)
    } catch {
      parsed = parsed.split(',')
    }
  }

  if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return []

  return [...new Set(parsed.map(normalizeKey).filter(Boolean))].sort()
}

function parseMatrixAnswer(value: any): Record<string, string> {
  let parsed = value

  if (typeof parsed === 'string') {
    try {
      parsed = JSON.parse(parsed)
    } catch {
      return {}
    }
  }

  const result: Record<string, string> = {}

  if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
    parsed.forEach((item, index) => {
      const normalized = normalizeTruth(item)
      if (normalized) result[String(index)] = normalized
    })
    return result
  }

  if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') {
    Object.entries(parsed).forEach(([key, item]) => {
      const normalized = normalizeTruth(item)
      if (normalized) result[String(key)] = normalized
    })
  }

  return result
}

function getMatrixOptionKey(item: any, index: number): string {
  if (
    item &&
    typeof item === 'object' &&
    item.id !== undefined &&
    item.id !== null
  ) {
    return String(item.id)
  }

  return String(index)
}
function PracticeContent() {
  const params = useParams()
  const examId = params?.id as string
  const router = useRouter()

  const [exam, setExam] = useState<any>(null)
  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState<any[]>([])
  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState(0)
  const [skippedQuestionIds, setSkippedQuestionIds] = useState<string[]>([])
  const [isReviewingSkipped, setIsReviewingSkipped] = useState(false)
  const [reviewIndex, setReviewIndex] = useState(0)

  const [userAnswer, setUserAnswer] = useState<any>(null)
  const [isAnswered, setIsAnswered] = useState(false)
  const [isCorrect, setIsCorrect] = useState(false)

  const [totalXpEarned, setTotalXpEarned] = useState(0)
  const [streakCount, setStreakCount] = useState(0)
  const [answeredCount, setAnsweredCount] = useState(0)
  const [correctCount, setCorrectCount] = useState(0)

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [attemptId, setAttemptId] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [isFinishing, setIsFinishing] = useState(false)
  const [finishSummary, setFinishSummary] = useState<any>(null)
  const [configurationError, setConfigurationError] = useState('')

  const finishStartedRef = useRef(false)
  const startAttemptPromiseRef = useRef<{
  examId: string;
  promise: Promise<{
    attemptId: string | null;
    error: unknown;
  }>;
} | null>(null);
  const streakXpRef = useRef(0)

  const checkAndUnlockBadges = async (
    currentXp: number,
    currentStreak: number
  ) => {
    const supabase = createClient()

    const {
      data: { user },
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser()

    if (!user) return

    const { data: profile } = await supabase
      .from('profiles')
      .select('badges')
      .eq('id', user.id)
      .single()

    const userBadges: string[] = Array.isArray(profile?.badges)
      ? profile.badges
      : []

    const newlyUnlocked: string[] = []

    if (!userBadges.includes('Langkah Pertama') && currentXp >= 10) {
      newlyUnlocked.push('Langkah Pertama')
    }

    if (!userBadges.includes('Raja Combo') && currentStreak >= 3) {
      newlyUnlocked.push('Raja Combo')
    }

    if (!userBadges.includes('Bintang Getasan') && currentXp >= 100) {
      newlyUnlocked.push('Bintang Getasan')
    }

    if (newlyUnlocked.length > 0) {
      await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .update({ badges: [...userBadges, ...newlyUnlocked] })
        .eq('id', user.id)

      alert(`🎉 Kamu membuka lencana: ${newlyUnlocked.join(', ')}!`)
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true

    async function loadQuestions() {
      if (!examId) return

      const supabase = createClient()

      const { data: examData, error: examError } = await supabase
        .from('exams')
        .select('*')
        .eq('id', examId)
        .single()

      if (examError || !examData) {
        alert('Modul latihan tidak ditemukan!')
        router.push('/practice')
        return
      }

      const { data: questionData, error: questionError } = await supabase
        .from('questions')
        .select('*')
        .eq('exam_id', examId)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: true })

      if (questionError) {
        alert('Gagal memuat soal latihan.')
        router.push('/practice')
        return
      }

      const loadedQuestions = questionData || []

      if (!active) return

      setExam(examData)
      setQuestions(loadedQuestions)

      if (
        examData.activity_type !== 'MAZE_GAME' &&
        examData.activity_type !== 'MAZE_GAME_V2' &&
        examData.activity_type !== 'FLASHCARD'
      ) {
        if (loadedQuestions.length === 0) {
          setConfigurationError('Modul ini belum memiliki soal.')
          setLoading(false)
          return
        }

        if (
  !startAttemptPromiseRef.current ||
  startAttemptPromiseRef.current.examId !== examId
) {
  const promise = Promise.resolve(
  supabase.rpc('start_practice_attempt', {
    p_exam_id: examId,
  })
)
  .then(({ data, error }) => {
    const attemptId =
      typeof data === 'string'
        ? data
        : data?.attempt_id ?? data?.id ?? null;

    return {
      attemptId,
      error,
    };
  })
  .catch((error: unknown) => ({
    attemptId: null,
    error,
  }));

  startAttemptPromiseRef.current = {
    examId,
    promise,
  };
}

const startResult =
  await startAttemptPromiseRef.current.promise;

if (startResult.error || !startResult.attemptId) {
  console.error(
    'Gagal memulai practice attempt:',
    startResult.error
  );

  if (active) {
    setConfigurationError(
      'Sesi latihan gagal dimulai. Silakan muat ulang halaman.'
    );
    setLoading(false);
  }

  return;
}

if (active) {
  setAttemptId(startResult.attemptId);
}
}
      if (active) setLoading(false)
    }

    loadQuestions()

    return () => {
      active = false
    }
  }, [examId, router])

  const currentQuestion = isReviewingSkipped
  ? questions.find((question) => question.id === skippedQuestionIds[reviewIndex])
  : questions[currentIndex]

const qType = currentQuestion?.question_type || 'multiple_choice'

  const processXpAndStreak = (isUserRight: boolean) => {
    if (isAnswered) return
    
    setConfigurationError('')

    setIsAnswered(true)
    setIsCorrect(isUserRight)
    setAnsweredCount((previous) => previous + 1)

    if (isUserRight) {
      setCorrectCount((previous) => previous + 1)

      const newStreak = streakCount + 1
      setStreakCount(newStreak)

      const bonus =
        newStreak >= 10 ? 10 :
        newStreak >= 6 ? 8 :
        newStreak >= 4 ? 5 :
        newStreak >= 2 ? 2 : 0

      streakXpRef.current += bonus
      setTotalXpEarned((previous) => previous + 10 + bonus)

      checkAndUnlockBadges(totalXpEarned + 10, newStreak)
    } else {
      setStreakCount(0)
    }
  }

  const handleSingleChoiceSelect = (selectedOptKey: string) => {
    if (isAnswered || !currentQuestion) return

    setUserAnswer(selectedOptKey)

    const correctOpt = normalizeKey(
      currentQuestion.correct_option ??
      currentQuestion.correct_answer
    )

    if (!correctOpt) {
      setConfigurationError('Kunci jawaban pilihan ganda belum diatur.')
      return
    }

    processXpAndStreak(normalizeKey(selectedOptKey) === correctOpt)
  }

  const handleToggleComplexChoice = (optKey: string) => {
    if (isAnswered) return

    const currentList: string[] = Array.isArray(userAnswer)
      ? userAnswer
      : []

    if (currentList.includes(optKey)) {
      setUserAnswer(currentList.filter((key: string) => key !== optKey))
    } else {
      setUserAnswer([...currentList, optKey])
    }
  }

  const handleCheckComplexAnswer = () => {
    if (isAnswered || !currentQuestion) return

    const correctList = parseComplexAnswer(
      currentQuestion.correct_answers ??
      currentQuestion.correct_option
    )

    if (correctList.length === 0) {
      setConfigurationError(
        'Kunci PG kompleks belum valid. Gunakan array seperti ["A","C"].'
      )
      return
    }

    const userList = parseComplexAnswer(userAnswer)

    const isUserRight =
      userList.length === correctList.length &&
      userList.every((value, index) => value === correctList[index])

    setConfigurationError('')
    processXpAndStreak(isUserRight)
  }

  const handleSelectMatrixRow = (optionKey: string, value: string) => {
  if (isAnswered) return

  const currentMatrix =
    userAnswer && typeof userAnswer === 'object'
      ? { ...userAnswer }
      : {}

  currentMatrix[optionKey] = value
  setUserAnswer(currentMatrix)
}

const handleCheckMatrixAnswer = () => {
  if (isAnswered || !currentQuestion) return

  const options = Array.isArray(currentQuestion.options)
    ? currentQuestion.options
    : []

  if (options.length === 0) {
    setConfigurationError('Pernyataan matriks belum tersedia.')
    return
  }

  const correctMap = parseMatrixAnswer(
    currentQuestion.correct_answers ??
    currentQuestion.correct_answer
  )

  const missingKeys: { key: string; row: number }[] = options
  .map((item: any, index: number) => ({
    key: getMatrixOptionKey(item, index),
    row: index + 1,
  }))
  .filter(
    ({ key }: { key: string; row: number }) => !correctMap[key]
  )

  if (missingKeys.length > 0) {
    setConfigurationError(
      `Kunci matriks belum lengkap. Periksa baris: ${missingKeys
        .map(({ row }) => row)
        .join(', ')}.`
    )
    return
  }

  const allCorrect = options.every((item: any, index: number) => {
    const key = getMatrixOptionKey(item, index)
    const userValue = normalizeTruth(userAnswer?.[key])

    return userValue !== null && userValue === correctMap[key]
  })

  setConfigurationError('')
  processXpAndStreak(allCorrect)
}

  const handleCheckFillInAnswer = () => {
    if (isAnswered || !currentQuestion) return

    const userText = String(userAnswer || '').trim().toLowerCase()
    const targetText = String(
      currentQuestion.correct_option ??
      currentQuestion.correct_answer ??
      ''
    ).trim().toLowerCase()

    if (!targetText) {
      setConfigurationError('Kunci jawaban isian belum diatur.')
      return
    }

    processXpAndStreak(userText === targetText)
  }

  const handleSubmitEssayAnswer = () => {
    if (isAnswered || !currentQuestion) return

    // Sesuai perilaku lama: essay mendapatkan XP berbasis usaha.
    processXpAndStreak(true)
  }

  const handleNextQuestion = () => {
  if (isReviewingSkipped) {
    if (!currentQuestion?.id) return

    setSkippedQuestionIds((previous) =>
      previous.filter((id) => id !== currentQuestion.id)
    )
    setReviewIndex(0)
  } else if (currentIndex < questions.length - 1) {
    setCurrentIndex((previous) => previous + 1)
  } else if (skippedQuestionIds.length > 0) {
    setIsReviewingSkipped(true)
    setReviewIndex(0)
  } else {
    return
  }

  setIsAnswered(false)
  setUserAnswer(null)
  setIsCorrect(false)
  setConfigurationError('')
}

  const handleSkipQuestion = () => {
  if (isAnswered || !currentQuestion?.id) return

  setStreakCount(0)
  setUserAnswer(null)
  setIsCorrect(false)
  setConfigurationError('')

  if (!isReviewingSkipped) {
    const nextSkippedIds = [
      ...new Set([...skippedQuestionIds, currentQuestion.id]),
    ]

    setSkippedQuestionIds(nextSkippedIds)

    if (currentIndex < questions.length - 1) {
      setCurrentIndex((previous) => previous + 1)
    } else {
      setIsReviewingSkipped(true)
      setReviewIndex(0)
    }

    return
  }

  // Saat putaran soal tertunda, pindahkan soal yang dilewati
  // ke urutan paling belakang jika masih ada soal tertunda lainnya.
  if (skippedQuestionIds.length > 1) {
  setSkippedQuestionIds((previous) => [
    ...previous.filter((id) => id !== currentQuestion.id),
    currentQuestion.id,
  ])
  setReviewIndex(0)
} else {
  setConfigurationError(
    'Soal ini masih tertunda. Kamu bisa mencoba menjawabnya atau memilih Selesai & Ambil XP.'
  )
}
}

  const handleFinishQuiz = async () => {
    if (!attemptId || finishStartedRef.current || isFinishing) return

    finishStartedRef.current = true
    setIsFinishing(true)

    const supabase = createClient()

    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc(
      'finish_practice_attempt',
      {
        p_attempt_id: attemptId,
        p_answered_count: answeredCount,
        p_correct_count: correctCount,
        p_streak_xp: streakXpRef.current,
      }
    )

    if (error) {
      console.error('Gagal menyimpan hasil:', error)
      alert('Hasil belum berhasil disimpan. Silakan coba lagi.')
      finishStartedRef.current = false
      setIsFinishing(false)
      return
    }

    setFinishSummary(data)
    setIsFinishing(false)
  }

  const comboMessages: Record<number, string> = {
    2: 'Mantap! Teruskan! 🔥',
    3: 'Hebat! Kamu mulai panas! ⚡',
    4: 'Luar biasa! Pertahankan! 🚀',
    5: 'Amazing! Kamu hebat sekali! 🌟',
    6: 'Super! Konsentrasimu luar biasa! 💪',
    7: 'Fantastis! Jangan berhenti! 🏆',
    8: 'Wow! Kamu sedang bersinar! ✨',
    9: 'Hampir sempurna! Ayo lanjut! 🔥',
    10: 'LEGENDARIS! 10 COMBO! 👑',
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-center">
        <p className="font-semibold text-sm">Memuat latihan...</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (exam?.activity_type === 'MAZE_GAME') {
    return <MazeGameEngine exam={exam} questions={questions} />
  }

  if (exam?.activity_type === 'FLASHCARD') {
    return <FlashcardEngine exam={exam} questions={questions} />
  }

  if (configurationError && !currentQuestion) {
    return <div className="p-8">{configurationError}</div>
  }

  if (!currentQuestion) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center">
        <p>{configurationError || 'Soal tidak tersedia.'}</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-100 flex flex-col font-sans select-none">
      <header className="bg-slate-900 text-white px-6 py-3.5 flex justify-between items-center shadow-md">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
          <Link
            href="/practice"
            className="px-3 py-1 bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl"
          >
            ← Keluar
          </Link>

          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] bg-indigo-500 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold">
              MODE LATIHAN MANDIRI
            </span>
            <h1 className="text-sm font-bold mt-1">{exam?.title}</h1>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="text-right">
          {streakCount >= 2 && (
            <div className="text-amber-300 text-xs font-black animate-pulse">
              🔥 Combo x{streakCount}!
            </div>
          )}

          {streakCount >= 2 && (
            <div className="text-amber-200 text-[10px] mt-1">
              {comboMessages[streakCount] ||
                (streakCount > 10
                  ? 'GILA KEREN! COMBO TERUS! 🎉'
                  : '')}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="text-amber-400 font-mono font-bold">
            ⭐ +{totalXpEarned} XP
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main className="flex-1 max-w-4xl w-full mx-auto p-4 sm:p-6">
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border shadow-sm space-y-6">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-b pb-3">
            <div>
              <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 text-xs">
              {isReviewingSkipped
                ? `Soal Tertunda ${reviewIndex + 1} dari ${skippedQuestionIds.length}`
                : `Soal ${currentIndex + 1} dari ${questions.length}`}              
              </span>
              <span className="ml-2 text-[10px] bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 px-2 py-1 rounded font-bold">
                {qType}
              </span>
            </div>

            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold">
              Benar: {correctCount} / {answeredCount} dijawab
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="text-slate-900 text-sm font-semibold leading-relaxed">
            <MathRenderer content={currentQuestion.question_text || ''} />
          </div>

          {currentQuestion.image_url && (
            <img
              src={currentQuestion.image_url}
              alt="Gambar soal"
              className="max-h-56 rounded-xl border"
            />
          )}

          {qType === 'multiple_choice' &&
            Array.isArray(currentQuestion.options) && (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {currentQuestion.options.map((opt: any, index: number) => {
                  const key = String(
                    opt?.key || String.fromCharCode(65 + index)
                  )
                  const correctKey = normalizeKey(
                    currentQuestion.correct_option ??
                    currentQuestion.correct_answer
                  )

                  const selected = userAnswer === key
                  const correct = normalizeKey(key) === correctKey

                  let style =
                    'border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-800'

                  if (isAnswered && correct) {
                    style =
                      'border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-900'
                  } else if (isAnswered && selected && !correct) {
                    style =
                      'border-rose-500 bg-rose-50 text-rose-900'
                  }

                  return (
                    <button
                      key={key}
                      disabled={isAnswered}
                      onClick={() => handleSingleChoiceSelect(key)}
                      className={`w-full text-left p-3.5 rounded-xl border text-xs ${style}`}
                    >
                      <span className="font-bold mr-3">{key}.</span>
                      <MathRenderer
                        content={
                          typeof opt === 'string'
                            ? opt
                            : opt.text || opt.title || ''
                        }
                      />
                    </button>
                  )
                })}
              </div>
            )}

          {qType === 'complex_multiple_choice' &&
            Array.isArray(currentQuestion.options) && (
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {currentQuestion.options.map((opt: any, index: number) => {
                  const key = String(
                    opt?.key || String.fromCharCode(65 + index)
                  )
                  const checked =
                    Array.isArray(userAnswer) && userAnswer.includes(key)

                  return (
                    <label
                      key={key}
                      className="flex items-center gap-3 border rounded-xl p-3 cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        disabled={isAnswered}
                        checked={checked}
                        onChange={() => handleToggleComplexChoice(key)}
                      />
                      <span className="text-sm font-semibold">
                        <MathRenderer
                          content={
                            typeof opt === 'string'
                              ? opt
                              : opt.text || opt.title || ''
                          }
                        />
                      </span>
                    </label>
                  )
                })}

                {!isAnswered && (
                  <button
                    onClick={handleCheckComplexAnswer}
                    className="w-full py-2.5 bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl"
                  >
                    ☑️ Cek Jawaban PG Kompleks
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

          {qType === 'true_false_matrix' &&
  Array.isArray(currentQuestion.options) && (
    <div className="border rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
      <table className="w-full text-xs">
        <thead className="bg-slate-100">
          <tr>
            <th className="p-3 text-left">Pernyataan</th>
            <th className="p-3">Benar</th>
            <th className="p-3">Salah</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>

        <tbody>
          {currentQuestion.options.map((item: any, index: number) => {
            const optionKey = getMatrixOptionKey(item, index)

            const text =
              typeof item === 'string'
                ? item
                : item.text ||
                  item.statement ||
                  `Pernyataan ${index + 1}`

            const selected = userAnswer?.[optionKey]

            return (
              <tr key={optionKey} className="border-t">
                <td className="p-3">
                  <MathRenderer content={text} />
                </td>

                <td className="p-3 text-center">
                  <button
                    disabled={isAnswered}
                    onClick={() =>
                      handleSelectMatrixRow(optionKey, 'Benar')
                    }
                    className={`px-3 py-1 rounded border ${
                      selected === 'Benar'
                        ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white'
                        : ''
                    }`}
                  >
                    Benar
                  </button>
                </td>

                <td className="p-3 text-center">
                  <button
                    disabled={isAnswered}
                    onClick={() =>
                      handleSelectMatrixRow(optionKey, 'Salah')
                    }
                    className={`px-3 py-1 rounded border ${
                      selected === 'Salah'
                        ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white'
                        : ''
                    }`}
                  >
                    Salah
                  </button>
                </td>
              </tr>
            )
          })}
        </tbody>
      </table>

      {!isAnswered && (
        <button
          onClick={handleCheckMatrixAnswer}
          className="w-full py-2.5 bg-indigo-600 text-white font-bold"
        >
          📊 Periksa Jawaban Matriks
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  )}

          {qType === 'fill_in' && (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <input
                type="text"
                disabled={isAnswered}
                value={userAnswer || ''}
                onChange={(event) => setUserAnswer(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="Ketik jawaban di sini..."
                className="w-full p-3 border rounded-xl text-sm"
              />

              {!isAnswered && (
                <button
                  onClick={handleCheckFillInAnswer}
                  className="w-full py-2.5 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl"
                >
                  Cek Jawaban
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {qType === 'essay' && (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <textarea
                rows={4}
                disabled={isAnswered}
                value={userAnswer || ''}
                onChange={(event) => setUserAnswer(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="Tuliskan jawaban lengkap..."
                className="w-full p-3 border rounded-xl text-sm"
              />

              {!isAnswered && (
                <button
                  onClick={handleSubmitEssayAnswer}
                  className="w-full py-2.5 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl"
                >
                  Kirim Jawaban
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {configurationError && (
            <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-amber-800 text-xs">
              {configurationError}
            </div>
          )}

          {isAnswered && (
            <div
              className={`p-4 rounded-xl border text-xs space-y-2 ${
                isCorrect
                  ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-300 text-emerald-900'
                  : 'bg-rose-50 border-rose-300 text-rose-900'
              }`}
            >
              <strong>
                {isCorrect ? '🎉 Jawaban benar!' : '❌ Jawaban belum tepat.'}
              </strong>

              {(currentQuestion.explanation ||
                currentQuestion.pembahasan) && (
                <div className="pt-2 border-t">
                  <strong>Pembahasan:</strong>
                  <MathRenderer
                    content={
                      currentQuestion.explanation ||
                      currentQuestion.pembahasan
                    }
                  />
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t pt-4">
  {!isAnswered && (
    <button
      onClick={handleSkipQuestion}
      className="rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-gray-100"
    >
      {isReviewingSkipped ? 'Lewati Kembali' : 'Lewati Soal'}
    </button>
  )}

  {isReviewingSkipped && (
    <button
      onClick={handleFinishQuiz}
      disabled={isFinishing || !attemptId}
      className="rounded-lg bg-green-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-green-700 disabled:opacity-50"
    >
      {isFinishing ? 'Menyimpan...' : '🏆 Selesai & Ambil XP'}
    </button>
  )}

  {isAnswered && !isReviewingSkipped && currentIndex < questions.length - 1 && (
    <button
      onClick={handleNextQuestion}
      className="rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700"
    >
      Soal Selanjutnya →
    </button>
  )}

  {isAnswered && !isReviewingSkipped && currentIndex === questions.length - 1 && (
    skippedQuestionIds.length > 0 ? (
      <button
        onClick={handleNextQuestion}
        className="rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700"
      >
        Lihat Soal Tertunda →
      </button>
    ) : (
      <button
        onClick={handleFinishQuiz}
        disabled={isFinishing || !attemptId}
        className="rounded-lg bg-green-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-green-700 disabled:opacity-50"
      >
        {isFinishing ? 'Menyimpan...' : '🏆 Selesai & Ambil XP'}
      </button>
    )
  )}

  {isAnswered && isReviewingSkipped && skippedQuestionIds.length > 1 && (
    <button
      onClick={handleNextQuestion}
      className="rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700"
    >
      Soal Tertunda Berikutnya →
    </button>
  )}
</div>
        </div>
      </main>

      {finishSummary && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/70 p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 text-center text-slate-900">
            <div className="text-4xl">🏆</div>
            <h2 className="mt-3 text-xl font-extrabold">Latihan Selesai!</h2>

            <p className="mt-4 text-4xl font-black text-indigo-600">
              {finishSummary.score ?? 0}
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500">NILAI AKHIR</p>

            <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm">
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-100 p-3">
                <p className="text-slate-500">Jawaban benar</p>
                <p className="text-xl font-extrabold">
                  {finishSummary.correct_count ?? correctCount}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-amber-50 p-3">
                <p className="text-amber-700">Total XP</p>
                <p className="text-xl font-extrabold text-amber-600">
                  +{finishSummary.total_xp ?? 0}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={() => router.push('/practice')}
              className="mt-6 w-full rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-4 py-3 font-bold text-white"
            >
              Kembali ke Latihan
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export default function InteractivePracticePage() {
  return (
    <Suspense
      fallback={
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-center">
          Memuat Lembar Latihan Interaktif...
        </div>
      }
    >
      <PracticeContent />
    </Suspense>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useEffect, useState, Suspense } from 'react'
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'

import MathRenderer from '@/components/MathRenderer'
import Link from 'next/link'

// 🖼️ KOMPONEN SMART LOGO BERBASIS REACT STATE
function SmartLogo({
  candidates,
  alt,
  fallbackText,
  className = 'w-16 h-16 object-contain shrink-0',
  fallbackClassName = 'w-16 h-16 bg-indigo-700 text-white font-black text-xl flex items-center justify-center rounded-2xl shrink-0',
}: {
  candidates: string[]
  alt: string
  fallbackText: string
  className?: string
  fallbackClassName?: string
}) {
  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState(0)
  const [failedAll, setFailedAll] = useState(false)

  const handleImgError = () => {
    if (currentIndex < candidates.length - 1) {
      setCurrentIndex((prev) => prev + 1)
    } else {
      setFailedAll(true)
    }
  }

  if (failedAll) {
    return <div className={fallbackClassName}>{fallbackText}</div>
  }

  return (
    <img
      src={candidates[currentIndex]}
      alt={alt}
      className={className}
      onError={handleImgError}
    />
  )
}

// 🟢 HELPER EVALUASI MULTI-TIPE SOAL
function evaluateAnswer(q: any, rawAns: any) {
  if (rawAns === null || rawAns === undefined || rawAns === '') {
    return { isCorrect: false, isUnanswered: true, isEssay: q.question_type === 'essay' || q.question_type === 'uraian', ratio: 0, userChoices: [], displayVal: 'Kosong' }
  }

  let parsed = rawAns
  if (typeof rawAns === 'string') {
    const trimmed = rawAns.trim()
    if (
      (trimmed.startsWith('[') && trimmed.endsWith(']')) ||
      (trimmed.startsWith('{') && trimmed.endsWith('}')) ||
      (trimmed.startsWith('"') && trimmed.endsWith('"'))
    ) {
      try {
        parsed = JSON.parse(trimmed)
      } catch (e) {
        parsed = trimmed
      }
    }
  }

  const qType = q.question_type || 'multiple_choice'

  if (qType === 'essay' || qType === 'uraian') {
    const textAns = typeof parsed === 'string' ? parsed.trim() : String(parsed || '').trim()
    const isAnswered = textAns.length > 0 && textAns !== 'null' && textAns !== 'undefined'
    return {
      isCorrect: false,
      isUnanswered: !isAnswered,
      isEssay: true,
      ratio: 0,
      userChoices: isAnswered ? [textAns] : [],
      displayVal: isAnswered ? textAns : 'Kosong'
    }
  }

  if (qType === 'multiple_choice') {
    const correctOpt = String(q.correct_option ?? q.correct_answer ?? q.correct_key ?? q.answer ?? '').trim().toUpperCase()
    const userChoice = String(parsed || '').trim().toUpperCase()
    const isCorrect = userChoice !== '' && userChoice === correctOpt
    return {
      isCorrect,
      isUnanswered: !userChoice,
      isEssay: false,
      ratio: isCorrect ? 1 : 0,
      userChoices: userChoice ? [userChoice] : [],
      displayVal: userChoice || 'Kosong'
    }
  }

  if (qType === 'complex_multiple_choice') {
    let correctKeys: string[] = []
    if (Array.isArray(q.correct_answers)) {
      correctKeys = q.correct_answers.map((k: any) => String(k).trim().toUpperCase())
    } else if (q.correct_option || q.correct_answer) {
      const str = String(q.correct_option ?? q.correct_answer)
      correctKeys = str.split(',').map((k) => k.trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean)
    }

    let userKeys: string[] = []
    if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
      userKeys = parsed.map((k: any) => String(k).trim().toUpperCase())
    } else if (typeof parsed === 'string' && parsed) {
      userKeys = parsed.split(',').map((k) => k.trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean)
    }

    if (userKeys.length === 0) {
      return { isCorrect: false, isUnanswered: true, isEssay: false, ratio: 0, userChoices: [], displayVal: 'Kosong' }
    }

    const correctUserChoices = userKeys.filter((k) => correctKeys.includes(k)).length
    const wrongUserChoices = userKeys.filter((k) => !correctKeys.includes(k)).length
    const ratio =
      correctKeys.length > 0
        ? Math.max(0, correctUserChoices - wrongUserChoices) / correctKeys.length
        : 0

    return {
      isCorrect: ratio === 1,
      isUnanswered: false,
      isEssay: false,
      ratio,
      userChoices: userKeys,
      displayVal: userKeys.join(', ')
    }
  }

  if (qType === 'true_false_matrix') {
    const correctObj: { [key: string]: string } = q.correct_answers || {}
    const userObj = typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed !== null ? parsed : {}

    const statementIds = Object.keys(correctObj)
    if (statementIds.length === 0) return { isCorrect: false, isUnanswered: true, isEssay: false, ratio: 0, userChoices: [], displayVal: 'Kosong' }

    let correctRows = 0
    let attemptedRows = 0
    statementIds.forEach((sId) => {
      if (userObj[sId]) attemptedRows++
      if (String(userObj[sId]).trim().toLowerCase() === String(correctObj[sId]).trim().toLowerCase()) {
        correctRows++
      }
    })

    if (attemptedRows === 0) return { isCorrect: false, isUnanswered: true, isEssay: false, ratio: 0, userChoices: [], displayVal: 'Kosong' }

    const ratio = correctRows / statementIds.length
    return {
      isCorrect: ratio === 1,
      isUnanswered: false,
      isEssay: false,
      ratio,
      userChoices: Object.values(userObj).map((v: any) => String(v).trim()),
      displayVal: `Benar ${correctRows}/${statementIds.length}`
    }
  }

  return { isCorrect: false, isUnanswered: true, isEssay: false, ratio: 0, userChoices: [], displayVal: 'Kosong' }
}

// 🟢 INTERFACE PENGATURAN DOKUMEN & TANDA TANGAN

  // State Modal Review & Penilaian Guru (Essay + Bonus + Override)

  'all' | 'wrong' | 'low' | 'ungraded' | 'maximum'
  >('all')
    // Posisi peserta yang sedang dinilai
  const [gradingSessionIndex, setGradingSessionIndex] = useState(0)
    // Posisi soal uraian yang sedang dinilai pada Mode per Soal
  const [gradingQuestionIndex, setGradingQuestionIndex] = useState(0)
  
// STATE KHUSUS MODE PENILAIAN PER SOAL
const [questionScores, setQuestionScores] = useState<Record<string, number>>({})
const [questionGradingHasChanges, setQuestionGradingHasChanges] = useState(false)
const [isSavingQuestionScores, setIsSavingQuestionScores] = useState(false)

  // Panel pengaturan nilai akhir
  const [showScoreSettings, setShowScoreSettings] = useState(false)
  const [sessionAnswersMap, setSessionAnswersMap] = useState<{ [qId: string]: any }>({})
  const [essayScores, setEssayScores] = useState<{ [qId: string]: number }>({})

  const [customFinalScore, setCustomFinalScore] = useState<number>(0)
  const [isSavingScore, setIsSavingScore] = useState<boolean>(false)
  const [gradingHasChanges, setGradingHasChanges] = useState(false)

  // State Cetak Sertifikat Single
  const [showPrintCertModal, setShowPrintCertModal] = useState<boolean>(false)

  // 🟢 STATE PENGATURAN DOKUMEN & TANDA TANGAN
  const [docSettings, setDocSettings] = useState<DocumentSettings>({
    schoolName: 'SD NEGERI GETASAN',
    schoolAddress: 'Jl. P. Diponegoro Km 6, Desa Getasan, Kab. Semarang (50774)',
    certificateTitle: 'SERTIFIKAT CAPAIAN AKADEMIK',
    issuePlace: 'Getasan',
    issueDate: new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { dateStyle: 'full' }),
    kkmTarget: 70,
    signatoryType: 'both',
    includeParentSign: true,
    principalName: 'GIYANTO, S.Pd.',
    principalNip: '19670715 199903 1 006',
    teacherName: 'Hasan Askari, S.Pd.',
    teacherNip: '19880520 201201 1 002',
  })

  const SD_LOGO_CANDIDATES = [
    '/logo-sd-getasan.png',
    '/logo-sd-getasan.PNG',
    '/logo-sd-getasan.jpg',
    '/logo-sd-getasan.jpeg',
    '/logo-sd.png',
  ]

  const TKA_LOGO_CANDIDATES = [
    '/logo-tka.png',
    '/logo-tka.PNG',
    '/logo-tka.jpg',
    '/logo-tka.jpeg',
    '/logo-kemendikbud.png',
  ]

  // =========================================================================
  // 📌 FUNGSI UTAMA: LOAD DATA (MENGAMBIL & MENGOLAH DATA DARI SUPABASE)
  // =========================================================================
  const loadData = async () => {
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 🔹 SEKSI 1: VALIDASI KONEKSI & INISIALISASI SUPABASE
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    if (!examId) return // Pembatalan jika ID Ujian tidak ditemukan di URL
    const supabase = createClient() // Inisialisasi client Supabase

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 🔹 SEKSI 2: QUERY DATA UTAMA DARI DATABASE (PARALEL/BERURUTAN)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. Ambil Informasi/Metadata Ujian (Judul, Subtes, dll)
    const { data: examData } = await supabase
      .from('exams')
      .select('*')
      .eq('id', examId)
      .single()

    // 2. Ambil Daftar Soal Ujian (Diurutkan berdasarkan waktu pembuatan)
    const { data: questionData } = await supabase
      .from('questions')
      .select('*')
      .eq('exam_id', examId)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: true })

    // 3. Ambil Sesi Pengerjaan Siswa (Diurutkan dari siswa yang terbaru selesai)
    const { data: sessionData } = await supabase
      .from('exam_sessions')
      .select('*')
      .eq('exam_id', examId)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })

    // 4. Ambil Profil Seluruh Siswa (Untuk pencocokan Nama & Kelas)
    const { data: profileData } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('*')

    // 5. Ambil Seluruh Jawaban Siswa dari Tabel `answers` berdasarkan Session IDs
    const sessionIds = (sessionData || []).map((s) => s.id)
    const { data: answerData } = sessionIds.length > 0
      ? await supabase.from('answers').select('*').in('session_id', sessionIds)
      : { data: [] }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 🔹 SEKSI 3: PEMBUATAN STRUKTUR PETA MEMORI (MAP LOOKUP O(1))
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Memetakan Data Profil berdasarkan `user_id` agar pencarian data siswa cepat
    const profileMap = new Map()
    if (profileData) {
      profileData.forEach((p) => profileMap.set(p.id, p))
    }

    // Memetakan Rincian Jawaban berdasarkan `session_id` pengerjaan
    const answerMap = new Map<string, any[]>()
    if (answerData) {
      answerData.forEach((ans) => {
        if (!answerMap.has(ans.session_id)) {
          answerMap.set(ans.session_id, [])
        }
        answerMap.get(ans.session_id)?.push(ans)
      })
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 🔹 SEKSI 4: PEMROSESAN NILAI & PERHITUNGAN SESI SISWA (PROCESSED SESSIONS)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    const processedSessions = (sessionData || []).map((session) => {
      // a. Ambil profil siswa (Gunakan Fallback default jika siswa anonim/tamu)
      const student = profileMap.get(session.user_id) || {
        full_name: 'Siswa / Tamu',
        username: 'guest',
        class_code: '-',
      }

      // b. Ambil rincian jawaban siswa untuk sesi ini
      const userAnswers = answerMap.get(session.id) || []
      const totalQ = questionData ? questionData.length : 0

      let correctCount = 0      // Penampung jumlah soal benar
      let totalPointsEarned = 0 // Penampung total poin yang didapat
      let totalMaxPoints = 0    // Penampung batas maksimal poin seluruh soal

      // c. Evaluasi Per Soal secara Real-Time
      if (questionData && questionData.length > 0) {
        questionData.forEach((q) => {
          const qMaxScore = Number(q.max_score) || 1
          totalMaxPoints += qMaxScore

          const ans = userAnswers.find((a) => a.question_id === q.id)
          const rawChoice = ans ? (ans.user_answer ?? ans.selected_answers ?? ans.selected_option) : null
          const evalRes = evaluateAnswer(q, rawChoice)

          const qType = q.question_type || 'multiple_choice'

          // Evaluasi Soal Uraian / Essay vs Pilihan Ganda (PG / PGK / Matriks)
          if (qType === 'essay' || qType === 'uraian') {
            const essayScore =
                ans?.teacher_score !== undefined && ans?.teacher_score !== null
                  ? Number(ans.teacher_score)
                  : 0
            totalPointsEarned += essayScore
            // Jika essay diberi skor > 0 oleh guru, hitung sebagai poin benar
            if (essayScore > 0) correctCount += 1
          } else {
            // Untuk pilihan ganda: jika benar penuh atau parsial (ratio >= 0.5)
            if (evalRes.isCorrect || evalRes.ratio >= 0.5) correctCount += 1
            totalPointsEarned += evalRes.ratio * qMaxScore
          }
        })
      }

      // d. Hitung Kalkulasi Nilai Akhir (Skala 0 - 100)
      const calculatedScore = totalMaxPoints > 0 ? Math.round((totalPointsEarned / totalMaxPoints) * 100) : 0
      
      // Utamakan skor tersimpan di Database `session.score` (jika pernah di-override / disimpan)
      const finalScore =
        session.score_override !== undefined && session.score_override !== null
          ? Number(session.score_override)
          : Math.min(
              100,
              Math.max(0, calculatedScore + (Number(session.bonus_points) || 0))
            )

      // e. 🟢 SINKRONISASI OTOMATIS (FALLBACK PERHITUNGAN JUMLAH BENAR)
      // Jika rincian di DB tidak ada/kosong tetapi Nilai Akhir ada, estimasikan jumlah benar dari Nilai Akhir
      if (correctCount === 0 && finalScore > 0 && totalQ > 0) {
        correctCount = Math.round((finalScore / 100) * totalQ)
      }

      const finishTime = session.end_time || session.submitted_at || session.completed_at || session.created_at

      // f. Kembalikan objek data siswa yang rapi untuk tabel rekapitulasi
      return {
        id: session.id,
        user_id: session.user_id,
        // Data pengaturan nilai yang tersimpan
        bonus_points: Number(session.bonus_points) || 0,
        score_override: session.score_override ?? null,

        full_name: student.full_name,
        username: student.username,
        class_code: student.class_code || '-',
        status: session.status || 'completed',
        completed_at: finishTime,
        correct_count: correctCount,
        total_questions: totalQ,
        earned_score: Math.round(finalScore),
        accuracy_percentage: Math.round(finalScore),
        rawAnswers: userAnswers,
      }
    })

    const totalParticipants = processedSessions.length

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 🔹 SEKSI 5: ANALISIS BUTIR SOAL, SEBARAN OPSI & DAYA BEDA (ITEM ANALYSIS)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    const analysisList = (questionData || []).map((q, qIdx) => {
      // 1. Urutkan siswa dari nilai tertinggi ke terendah untuk membagi Kelompok Atas & Bawah (27%)
      const sortedByScoreDesc = [...processedSessions].sort((a, b) => b.earned_score - a.earned_score)
      const N = sortedByScoreDesc.length
      const groupSize = Math.max(1, Math.floor(N * 0.27))
      const upperGroup = sortedByScoreDesc.slice(0, groupSize)
      const lowerGroup = sortedByScoreDesc.slice(Math.max(0, N - groupSize))

      let upperCorrect = 0
      let lowerCorrect = 0

      // Hitung berapa banyak siswa kelompok atas & bawah yang menjawab soal ini dengan benar
      if (answerData) {
        answerData.forEach((ans) => {
          if (ans.question_id === q.id) {
            const rawChoice = ans.user_answer ?? ans.selected_answers ?? ans.selected_option
            const { isCorrect } = evaluateAnswer(q, rawChoice)

            if (isCorrect) {
              if (upperGroup.some((s) => s.id === ans.session_id)) upperCorrect++
              if (lowerGroup.some((s) => s.id === ans.session_id)) lowerCorrect++
            }
          }
        })
      }

      // Rumus Indeks Daya Beda D = (UpperCorrect - LowerCorrect) / N_kelompok
      const discriminationIndexVal = groupSize > 0 ? (upperCorrect - lowerCorrect) / groupSize : 0
      const discriminationPercent = Math.round(discriminationIndexVal * 100)

      // 2. Hitung Sebaran Opsi Jawaban (A, B, C, D, Kosong) & Pengecoh Utama (Distraktor)
      let correctAnswersCount = 0
      let answeredCount = 0
      const optionCounts: { [key: string]: number } = { A: 0, B: 0, C: 0, D: 0, Kosong: 0 }

      let correctOptDisplay = ''
      if (q.question_type === 'complex_multiple_choice') {
        correctOptDisplay = Array.isArray(q.correct_answers) ? q.correct_answers.join(',') : String(q.correct_option ?? q.correct_answer ?? '').trim().toUpperCase()
      } else if (q.question_type === 'true_false_matrix') {
        correctOptDisplay = 'Matriks'
      } else if (q.question_type === 'essay' || q.question_type === 'uraian') {
        correctOptDisplay = 'ESSAY'
      } else {
        correctOptDisplay = String(q.correct_option ?? q.correct_answer ?? 'A').trim().toUpperCase()
      }

      if (answerData) {
        answerData.forEach((ans) => {
          if (ans.question_id === q.id) {
            const rawChoice = ans.user_answer ?? ans.selected_answers ?? ans.selected_option
            const { isCorrect, isUnanswered, userChoices } = evaluateAnswer(q, rawChoice)

            if (isCorrect) correctAnswersCount += 1
            if (!isUnanswered) answeredCount += 1

            if (userChoices.length > 0) {
              userChoices.forEach((choice) => {
                const upperChoice = String(choice).toUpperCase()
                if (['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'].includes(upperChoice)) {
                  optionCounts[upperChoice] = (optionCounts[upperChoice] || 0) + 1
                } else if (['BENAR', 'SALAH'].includes(upperChoice)) {
                  optionCounts[upperChoice] = (optionCounts[upperChoice] || 0) + 1
                }
              })
            } else {
              optionCounts['Kosong'] = (optionCounts['Kosong'] || 0) + 1
            }
          }
        })
      }

      const wrongAnswersCount = totalParticipants - correctAnswersCount
      const accuracyRate = totalParticipants > 0 ? Math.round((correctAnswersCount / totalParticipants) * 100) : 0

      // Cari opsi jawaban mana selain kunci yang paling banyak dipilih siswa (Distraktor Terkuat)
      let topDistractorKey = '-'
      let maxDistractorCount = 0

      Object.keys(optionCounts).forEach((optKey) => {
        if (!correctOptDisplay.includes(optKey) && optKey !== 'Kosong') {
          if (optionCounts[optKey] > maxDistractorCount) {
            maxDistractorCount = optionCounts[optKey]
            topDistractorKey = optKey
          }
        }
      })

      // 3. Klasifikasi Tingkat Kesulitan Soal Berdasarkan Persentase Akurasi Jawaban Benar
      let difficultyLabel = 'Sedang'
      let difficultyBadge = 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200'

      if (q.question_type === 'essay' || q.question_type === 'uraian') {
        difficultyLabel = 'Soal Essay'
        difficultyBadge = 'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 border-indigo-200'
      } else if (accuracyRate >= 80) {
        difficultyLabel = 'Sangat Mudah'
        difficultyBadge = 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200'
      } else if (accuracyRate >= 60) {
        difficultyLabel = 'Mudah'
        difficultyBadge = 'bg-teal-50 text-teal-700 border-teal-200'
      } else if (accuracyRate >= 40) {
        difficultyLabel = 'Sedang'
        difficultyBadge = 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200'
      } else {
        difficultyLabel = 'Sulit / Perlu Evaluasi'
        difficultyBadge = 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-200'
      }

      return {
        question_no: qIdx + 1,
        question_id: q.id,
        question_type: q.question_type || 'multiple_choice',
        question_text: q.question_text || '',
        correct_option: correctOptDisplay,
        max_score: q.max_score || 1,
        correct_count: correctAnswersCount,
        answeredCount,
        wrong_count: wrongAnswersCount,
        accuracy_rate: accuracyRate,
        discriminationPercent,
        optionCounts,
        topDistractorKey: maxDistractorCount > 0 ? topDistractorKey : 'Tidak ada',
        maxDistractorCount,
        difficultyLabel,
        difficultyBadge,
      }
    })

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 🔹 SEKSI 6: UPDATE STATE REACT HINGGA MEMATIKAN SPINNER LOADING
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    if (examData) setExam(examData)
    if (questionData) setQuestions(questionData)
    setSessions(processedSessions)
    setItemAnalysisList(analysisList)
    setLoading(false) // Mematikan status loading pada antarmuka
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // 🔹 SEKSI 7: TRIGGER AUTOMATIS SAAT HALAMAN DIBUKA / EXAM ID BERUBAH
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  useEffect(() => {
    loadData()
  }, [examId])

  const calculateAutoScore = (
    answersMap: { [qId: string]: any },
    currentEssayScores: { [qId: string]: number },
    bonus: number = 0
  ) => {
    let totalPoints = 0
    let maxPoints = 0

    questions.forEach((q) => {
      const qMax = Number(q.max_score) || 1
      maxPoints += qMax
      const qType = q.question_type || 'multiple_choice'

      if (qType === 'essay' || qType === 'uraian') {
        const manualScore =
        currentEssayScores[q.id] !== undefined
          ? Number(currentEssayScores[q.id])
          : 0
        totalPoints += Math.min(qMax, Math.max(0, manualScore))
      } else {
        const rawAns = answersMap[q.id]
        const evalRes = evaluateAnswer(q, rawAns)
        totalPoints += evalRes.ratio * qMax
      }
    })

    const baseScore = maxPoints > 0 ? Math.round((totalPoints / maxPoints) * 100) : 0
    return Math.min(100, Math.max(0, baseScore + bonus))
  }

const essayQuestions = questions.filter((q) => {
  const type = q.question_type || 'multiple_choice'
  return type === 'essay' || type === 'uraian'
})


const getFilteredGradingQuestions = () => {
  return questions.filter((q) => {
    const type = q.question_type || 'multiple_choice'
    const isEssay = type === 'essay' || type === 'uraian'
    const qMax = Number(q.max_score) || 1

    // FILTER JENIS SOAL
    if (gradingQuestionFilter === 'essay' && !isEssay) return false

    if (
      gradingQuestionFilter !== 'all' &&
      gradingQuestionFilter !== 'essay' &&
      type !== gradingQuestionFilter
    ) {
      return false
    }

    // CARI NILAI YANG SUDAH TERSIMPAN
    const savedAnswer = selectedSessionForDetail?.rawAnswers?.find(
      (ans: any) => String(ans.question_id) === String(q.id)
    )

    const hasSavedScore =
      savedAnswer?.teacher_score !== null &&
      savedAnswer?.teacher_score !== undefined

    // STATUS ESSAY
    if (isEssay) {
      if (gradingStatusFilter === 'ungraded') return !hasSavedScore
      if (gradingStatusFilter === 'all') return true
      if (!hasSavedScore) return false

      const score = Number(savedAnswer.teacher_score)

      if (gradingStatusFilter === 'wrong') return score === 0
      if (gradingStatusFilter === 'low') return score > 0 && score < qMax
      if (gradingStatusFilter === 'maximum') return score >= qMax

      return true
    }

    // STATUS SOAL OTOMATIS
    const rawAns = sessionAnswersMap[q.id]
    const evalRes = evaluateAnswer(q, rawAns)
    const ratio = Number(evalRes.ratio) || 0
    
    if (gradingStatusFilter === 'ungraded') return false
    
    // Jawaban kosong tidak dimasukkan ke kategori salah
    if (evalRes.isUnanswered) return false
    
    // Salah sepenuhnya
    if (gradingStatusFilter === 'wrong') {
      return ratio === 0
    }
    
    // Benar sebagian
    if (gradingStatusFilter === 'low') {
      return ratio > 0 && ratio < 1
    }
    
    // Benar sepenuhnya
    if (gradingStatusFilter === 'maximum') {
      return ratio >= 1
    }

    return true
  })
}


  const handleOpenDetailModal = (session: any) => {
    const map: { [key: string]: any } = {}
    const answerScoreMap: { [key: string]: number } = {}

    if (Array.isArray(session.rawAnswers)) {
      session.rawAnswers.forEach((ans: any) => {
        map[ans.question_id] = ans.user_answer ?? ans.selected_answers ?? ans.selected_option
        if (ans.teacher_score !== undefined && ans.teacher_score !== null) {
          answerScoreMap[ans.question_id] = Number(ans.teacher_score)
        }
      })
    }
    setSessionAnswersMap(map)

    const initialEssayScores: { [key: string]: number } = {}
      questions.forEach((q) => {
        const qType = q.question_type || 'multiple_choice'
      
        if (qType === 'essay' || qType === 'uraian') {
          const savedScore = answerScoreMap[q.id]
          const rawAnswer = map[q.id]
      
          const hasAnswer =
            rawAnswer !== null &&
            rawAnswer !== undefined &&
            (
              typeof rawAnswer === 'string'
                ? rawAnswer.trim() !== ''
                : Array.isArray(rawAnswer)
                  ? rawAnswer.length > 0
                  : typeof rawAnswer === 'object'
                    ? Object.values(rawAnswer).some(
                        (value: any) =>
                          value !== null &&
                          value !== undefined &&
                          String(value).trim() !== ''
                      )
                    : true
            )
      
          initialEssayScores[q.id] =
            savedScore !== undefined
              ? savedScore
              : hasAnswer
                ? 1
                : 0
        }
      })

    setEssayScores(initialEssayScores)
    const savedBonus = Number(session.bonus_points) || 0
    const overrideActive =
      session.score_override !== null &&
      session.score_override !== undefined
    
    setBonusPoints(savedBonus)
    setIsOverrideScore(overrideActive)
    const autoCalculated = calculateAutoScore(
      map,
      initialEssayScores,
      savedBonus
    )
    
    setCustomFinalScore(
      overrideActive
        ? Number(session.score_override)
        : autoCalculated
    )
    const currentIndex = sortedSessions.findIndex(
      (s) => s.id === session.id
    )
    
    if (currentIndex >= 0) {
      setGradingSessionIndex(currentIndex)
    }
    setSelectedSessionForDetail(session)
  }
const goToGradingSession = (nextIndex: number) => {
  if (nextIndex < 0 || nextIndex >= sortedSessions.length) return

  if (gradingHasChanges) {
    const confirmLeave = window.confirm(
      'Ada perubahan nilai yang belum disimpan. Jika berpindah peserta sekarang, perubahan tersebut akan hilang. Apakah Anda yakin ingin melanjutkan?'
    )

    if (!confirmLeave) return
  }

  const nextSession = sortedSessions[nextIndex]

  setGradingHasChanges(false)
  setGradingSessionIndex(nextIndex)
  handleOpenDetailModal(nextSession)
}
  
  const handleEssayScoreChange = (qId: string, value: number, maxScore: number) => {
    const val = Math.max(0, Math.min(value, maxScore))
    const updatedEssayScores = { ...essayScores, [qId]: val }
    setEssayScores(updatedEssayScores)
    setGradingHasChanges(true)

    if (!isOverrideScore) {
      const autoCalculated = calculateAutoScore(sessionAnswersMap, updatedEssayScores, bonusPoints)
      setCustomFinalScore(autoCalculated)
    }
  }

  const handleBonusChange = (bonusVal: number) => {
    const val = Number(bonusVal) || 0
    setBonusPoints(val)
    setGradingHasChanges(true)
    if (!isOverrideScore) {
      const autoCalculated = calculateAutoScore(sessionAnswersMap, essayScores, val)
      setCustomFinalScore(autoCalculated)
    }
  }

  const handleOverrideToggle = (checked: boolean) => {
    setIsOverrideScore(checked)
    setGradingHasChanges(true)
    if (!checked) {
      const autoCalculated = calculateAutoScore(sessionAnswersMap, essayScores, bonusPoints)
      setCustomFinalScore(autoCalculated)
    }
  }

  
const handleSaveAssessment = async () => {
  if (!selectedSessionForDetail) return
  setGradingHasChanges(false)
  setIsSavingScore(true)

  const supabase = createClient()
  const newScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(customFinalScore) || 0))

  try {
    // 1. Simpan nilai manual setiap soal uraian
    for (const [qId, eScore] of Object.entries(essayScores)) {
      const { error: answerError } = await supabase
        .from('answers')
        .update({ teacher_score: eScore })
        .eq('session_id', selectedSessionForDetail.id)
        .eq('question_id', qId)

      if (answerError) throw answerError
    }

    // 2. Simpan nilai akhir, bonus, dan override
    const { error: sessionError } = await supabase
      .from('exam_sessions')
      .update({
        score: newScore,
        bonus_points: bonusPoints,
        score_override: isOverrideScore ? newScore : null
      })
      .eq('id', selectedSessionForDetail.id)

    if (sessionError) throw sessionError

    // 3. Perbarui tampilan daftar hasil
    setSessions((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => {
        if (s.id === selectedSessionForDetail.id) {
          return {
            ...s,
            score: newScore,
            earned_score: newScore,
            accuracy_percentage: newScore,
            bonus_points: bonusPoints,
            score_override: isOverrideScore ? newScore : null
          }
        }
        return s
      })
    )

    alert(
  `✅ Nilai ${selectedSessionForDetail.full_name} berhasil diperbarui menjadi ${newScore}!`
    )
    
    setGradingHasChanges(false)
    setSelectedSessionForDetail(null)
    
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(`Gagal menyimpan nilai: ${e.message}`)
    } finally {
      setIsSavingScore(false)
    }
    }
// menampilkan nilai dan jawaban seluruh peserta
const getQuestionGradingData = () => {
  const currentQuestion = essayQuestions[gradingQuestionIndex]

  if (!currentQuestion) return []

  return sortedSessions.map((session) => {
    const answer = (session.rawAnswers || []).find(
      (ans: any) => ans.question_id === currentQuestion.id
    )

    const rawAnswer = answer
      ? answer.user_answer ?? answer.selected_answers ?? answer.selected_option
      : null

    const hasAnswer =
      rawAnswer !== null &&
      rawAnswer !== undefined &&
      (
        Array.isArray(rawAnswer)
          ? rawAnswer.some((item) => String(item).trim() !== '')
          : String(rawAnswer).trim() !== ''
      )

    const savedScore =
      answer?.teacher_score !== null &&
      answer?.teacher_score !== undefined
        ? Number(answer.teacher_score)
        : null

    return {
      sessionId: session.id,
      fullName: session.full_name,
      classCode: session.class_code,
      answerId: answer?.id ?? null,
      answerText: hasAnswer
        ? Array.isArray(rawAnswer)
          ? rawAnswer.join(', ')
          : String(rawAnswer)
        : '',
      hasAnswer,
      score: savedScore,
      maxScore: Number(currentQuestion.max_score) || 1,
    }
  })
}

const handleSaveAllQuestionScores = async () => {
  const currentQuestion = essayQuestions[gradingQuestionIndex]

  if (!currentQuestion) {
    alert('Tidak ada soal essay atau uraian yang dipilih.')
    return
  }

  if (questionGradingHasChanges === false) {
    alert('Belum ada perubahan nilai yang perlu disimpan.')
    return
  }

  const confirmSave = window.confirm(
    'Apakah Anda yakin ingin menyimpan seluruh nilai peserta untuk soal ini?'
  )

  if (!confirmSave) return

  setIsSavingQuestionScores(true)

  const supabase = createClient()

  try {
    for (const session of sortedSessions) {
      const answer = (session.rawAnswers || []).find(
        (ans: any) =>
          String(ans.question_id) === String(currentQuestion.id)
      )

      if (!answer) continue

      const rawAnswer =
        answer.user_answer ??
        answer.selected_answers ??
        answer.selected_option

      const hasAnswer =
        rawAnswer !== null &&
        rawAnswer !== undefined &&
        (
          Array.isArray(rawAnswer)
            ? rawAnswer.some((item: any) => String(item).trim() !== '')
            : String(rawAnswer).trim() !== ''
        )

      if (!hasAnswer) continue

      const scoreKey = `${session.id}_${currentQuestion.id}`

      const manualScore =
        questionScores[scoreKey] ??
        (answer.teacher_score !== null &&
        answer.teacher_score !== undefined
          ? Number(answer.teacher_score)
          : 0)

      const maxScore = Number(currentQuestion.max_score) || 1

      const safeScore = Math.max(
        0,
        Math.min(maxScore, Number(manualScore) || 0)
      )

      // 1. Simpan nilai essay pada tabel answers
      const { error: answerError } = await supabase
        .from('answers')
        .update({ teacher_score: safeScore })
        .eq('session_id', session.id)
        .eq('question_id', currentQuestion.id)

      if (answerError) throw answerError

      // 2. Siapkan seluruh nilai essay peserta
      const currentEssayScores: { [qId: string]: number } = {}

      essayQuestions.forEach((q) => {
        const savedAnswer = (session.rawAnswers || []).find(
          (ans: any) =>
            String(ans.question_id) === String(q.id)
        )

        if (
          String(q.id) === String(currentQuestion.id)
        ) {
          currentEssayScores[q.id] = safeScore
        } else if (
          savedAnswer?.teacher_score !== null &&
          savedAnswer?.teacher_score !== undefined
        ) {
          currentEssayScores[q.id] = Number(
            savedAnswer.teacher_score
          )
        } else {
          currentEssayScores[q.id] = 0
        }
      })

      // 3. Siapkan jawaban otomatis peserta
      const answersMap: { [qId: string]: any } = {}

      ;(session.rawAnswers || []).forEach((ans: any) => {
        answersMap[ans.question_id] =
          ans.user_answer ??
          ans.selected_answers ??
          ans.selected_option
      })

      // 4. Hitung nilai akhir
      const bonus = Number(session.bonus_points) || 0

      const calculatedScore = calculateAutoScore(
        answersMap,
        currentEssayScores,
        bonus
      )

      // Override tetap menjadi prioritas
      const finalScore =
        session.score_override !== null &&
        session.score_override !== undefined
          ? Number(session.score_override)
          : calculatedScore

      // 5. Perbarui nilai akhir peserta
      const { error: sessionError } = await supabase
        .from('exam_sessions')
        .update({ score: finalScore })
        .eq('id', session.id)

      if (sessionError) throw sessionError
    }

    // 6. Muat ulang data terbaru
    setQuestionScores({})
    setQuestionGradingHasChanges(false)

    await loadData()

    alert('Nilai seluruh peserta berhasil disimpan.')

  } catch (error: any) {
    alert(`Gagal menyimpan nilai: ${error.message}`)
  } finally {
    setIsSavingQuestionScores(false)
  }
}

  
  const handlePrint = () => {
    window.print()

  // 🗑️ METODE HAPUS SATUAN PER SISWA
  const handleDeleteSession = async (sessionId: string, studentName: string) => {
    if (!confirm(`Apakah Anda yakin ingin mereset/menghapus hasil ujian siswa "${studentName}"?`)) return
    setIsDeleting(true)
    const supabase = createClient()
    await supabase.from('answers').delete().eq('session_id', sessionId)
    await supabase.from('exam_sessions').delete().eq('id', sessionId)
    setSelectedSessionIds((prev) => prev.filter((id) => id !== sessionId))
    setIsDeleting(false)
    loadData()
  }

  // 🗑️ METODE HAPUS TERPILIH (BULK DELETE)
  const handleDeleteSelected = async () => {
    if (selectedSessionIds.length === 0) return
    if (!confirm(`Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus ${selectedSessionIds.length} hasil ujian terpilih?`)) return
    setIsDeleting(true)
    const supabase = createClient()
    await supabase.from('answers').delete().in('session_id', selectedSessionIds)
    await supabase.from('exam_sessions').delete().in('id', selectedSessionIds)
    setSelectedSessionIds([])
    setIsDeleting(false)
    loadData()
  }

  // 🗑️ METODE HAPUS SEMUA (RESET TOTAL)
  const handleDeleteAll = async () => {
    if (sessions.length === 0) return
    if (!confirm(`⚠️ PERINGATAN BERSAMA:\nHapus SELURUH (${sessions.length}) hasil pengerjaan siswa?`)) return
    setIsDeleting(true)
    const supabase = createClient()
    const allSessionIds = sessions.map((s) => s.id)
    if (allSessionIds.length > 0) {
      await supabase.from('answers').delete().in('session_id', allSessionIds)
      await supabase.from('exam_sessions').delete().eq('exam_id', examId)
    }
    setSelectedSessionIds([])
    setIsDeleting(false)
    loadData()
  }

  const handleSelectAll = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.target.checked) {
      setSelectedSessionIds(sortedSessions.map((s) => s.id))

      setSelectedSessionIds([])
    }
  }

  const handleSelectSession = (id: string) => {
    setSelectedSessionIds((prev) =>
      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((item) => item !== id) : [...prev, id]
    )
  }

  const filteredSessions = sessions.filter((s) => {
    const term = searchTerm.toLowerCase()
    const matchSearch =
      s.full_name.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
      s.username.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
      s.class_code.toLowerCase().includes(term)
    const matchClass = filterClass === 'ALL' || s.class_code === filterClass
    return matchSearch && matchClass
  })

  const uniqueClasses = Array.from(new Set(sessions.map((s) => s.class_code)))

  const sortedSessions = [...filteredSessions].sort((a, b) => {
    let valA: any = a.earned_score
    let valB: any = b.earned_score

    if (sortByStudent === 'name') {
      valA = a.full_name.toLowerCase()
      valB = b.full_name.toLowerCase()
    } else if (sortByStudent === 'class') {
      valA = a.class_code.toLowerCase()
      valB = b.class_code.toLowerCase()
    } else if (sortByStudent === 'date') {
      valA = new Date(a.completed_at || 0).getTime()
      valB = new Date(b.completed_at || 0).getTime()
    }

    if (valA < valB) return sortOrderStudent === 'asc' ? -1 : 1
    if (valA > valB) return sortOrderStudent === 'asc' ? 1 : -1
    return 0
  })

  const sortedItems = [...itemAnalysisList].sort((a, b) => {
    if (sortByItem === 'accuracy') {
      return sortOrderItem === 'asc' ? a.accuracy_rate - b.accuracy_rate : b.accuracy_rate - a.accuracy_rate
    }
    return sortOrderItem === 'asc' ? a.question_no - b.question_no : b.question_no - a.question_no
  })

  const totalParticipants = sessions.length
  const totalScores = sessions.map((s) => s.earned_score)
  const averageScore = totalParticipants > 0 ? (totalScores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / totalParticipants).toFixed(1) : '0'
  const highestScore = totalParticipants > 0 ? Math.max(...totalScores) : 0
  const lowestScore = totalParticipants > 0 ? Math.min(...totalScores) : 0

  const handleExportToExcel = () => {
    if (sessions.length === 0) return alert('Belum ada data pengerjaan siswa.')
    const workbook = XLSX.utils.book_new()
    const studentData = sortedSessions.map((s, idx) => ({
      No: idx + 1,
      'Nama Lengkap Siswa': s.full_name,
      Username: s.username,
      'Kode Kelas': s.class_code,
      'Waktu Selesai': s.completed_at ? new Date(s.completed_at).toLocaleString('id-ID') : '-',
      'Jawaban Benar': `${s.correct_count} / ${s.total_questions}`,
      'Nilai Akhir': s.earned_score,
    }))
    const wsStudents = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(studentData)
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(workbook, wsStudents, 'Rekap Nilai Siswa')

    const itemData = sortedItems.map((item) => ({
      'No Soal': item.question_no,
      'Teks Soal': (item.question_text || '').replace(/<[^>]*>?/gm, ''),
      'Kunci Jawaban': item.correct_option,
      'Akurasi (%)': `${item.accuracy_rate}%`,
      'Daya Beda (%)': `${item.discriminationPercent}%`,
      'Pengecoh Utama': item.topDistractorKey !== 'Tidak ada' ? `Opsi ${item.topDistractorKey}` : '-',
      'Kategori Kesulitan': item.difficultyLabel,
    }))
    const wsItems = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(itemData)
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(workbook, wsItems, 'Analisis Butir & Distraktor')

    const cleanTitle = (exam?.title || 'Ujian').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '_')
    XLSX.writeFile(workbook, `Rekap_Lengkap_${cleanTitle}.xlsx`)
  }

  // 🟢 KOMPONEN BLOK TANDA TANGAN KEKINIAN & ADAPTIF
  const DynamicSignatureBlock = ({ studentClass }: { studentClass?: string }) => {

            <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono mt-0.5">NIP. {docSettings.principalNip}</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    )
  }

  const dummyStudent = {
    full_name: 'Cristiano Ronaldo (Contoh Siswa)',
    username: 'cr7',
    class_code: '6A',
    earned_score: 95,
    correct_count: 19,
    total_questions: 20,
    completed_at: new Date().toISOString(),

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 font-sans">

  return (

        {/* HEADER HALAMAN */}

          <div>
            <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 text-xs font-bold rounded-full uppercase">
              {exam?.subtest}
            </span>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-800 mt-1">
              📊 Rekapitulasi & Desain Dokumen: {exam?.title}
            </h1>

              Kelola hasil ujian siswa, sesuaikan format penandatanganan dokumen, dan cetak sertifikat & rapor berdesain kekinian.
            </p>

          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <button
              onClick={handleExportToExcel}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition-colors shadow-sm flex items-center space-x-1.5"
            >
              <span>📊 Export (.xlsx)</span>
            </button>
            <Link
              href={`/admin/exam/${examId}/analysis`}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition-colors shadow-sm flex items-center space-x-1.5"
            >
              <span>🎯 Analisis Soal Detail</span>
            </Link>
            <Link
              href="/admin"
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-900 text-white text-xs font-medium rounded-lg transition-colors"
            >
              ← Kembali
            </Link>
            
          </div>
        </div>
        
        {/* TAB NAVIGASI UTAMA ADMIN */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 bg-slate-200 p-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 w-fit">
          <button
            onClick={() => setMainActiveTab('recap')}
            className={`px-5 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
              mainActiveTab === 'recap'
                ? 'bg-white text-indigo-700 shadow-sm'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
            }`}
          >
            📊 Data & Analisis Nilai ({sortedSessions.length})
          </button>
        
          <button
            onClick={() => setMainActiveTab('grading')}
            className={`px-5 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
              mainActiveTab === 'grading'
                ? 'bg-white text-indigo-700 shadow-sm'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
            }`}
          >
            📝 Penilaian Manual
          </button>
        
          <button
            onClick={() => setMainActiveTab('settings')}
            className={`px-5 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
              mainActiveTab === 'settings'
                ? 'bg-white text-indigo-700 shadow-sm'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
            }`}
          >
            ⚙️ Desain Penandatanganan & Live Preview
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* ========================================================================= */}
        {/* TAB 1: DATA REKAPITULASI & ANALISIS BUTIR                                 */}
        {/* ========================================================================= */}
        {mainActiveTab === 'recap' && (
          <div className="space-y-8">
            {/* STATISTIK RINGKAS */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
              <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm space-y-1">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase block">👥 Total Peserta</span>
                <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-800">{totalParticipants} Siswa</span>
              </div>
              <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm space-y-1">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase block">📈 Rata-Rata Nilai</span>
                <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-indigo-600">{averageScore}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm space-y-1">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase block">🏆 Nilai Tertinggi</span>
                <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-emerald-600">{highestScore}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm space-y-1">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase block">📉 Nilai Terendah</span>
                <span className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-rose-600">{lowestScore}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* TABEL ANALISIS SOAL */}
            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm overflow-hidden space-y-3 p-5">
              <div className="border-b pb-3 flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between sm:items-center gap-2">
                <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-800 flex items-center space-x-2">
                  <span>🎯</span>
                  <span>Analisis Butir Soal, Sebaran Opsi & Daya Beda ($D$)</span>
                </h2>
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 shrink-0">
                  <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">Urutkan:</span>
                  <select
                    value={sortByItem}
                    onChange={(e: any) => setSortByItem(e.target.value)}
                    className="px-2.5 py-1 border rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700"
                  >
                    <option value="no">Berdasarkan Nomor Soal</option>
                    <option value="accuracy">Berdasarkan Akurasi</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="overflow-x-auto pt-2">
                <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
                  <thead className="bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 font-bold border-b">
                    <tr>
                      <th className="p-3 text-center w-12">No</th>
                      <th className="p-3">Pertanyaan / Soal</th>
                      <th className="p-3 text-center">Kunci</th>
                      <th className="p-3 text-center w-48">Sebaran Opsi Jawaban</th>
                      <th className="p-3 text-center">Pengecoh Utama</th>
                      <th className="p-3 text-center w-32">Daya Beda ($D$)</th>
                      <th className="p-3 text-center w-36">Tingkat Akurasi</th>
                      <th className="p-3 text-center">Kesulitan</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                    {sortedItems.map((item) => (
                      <tr key={item.question_id} className="hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors">
                        <td className="p-3 text-center font-bold text-slate-700">{item.question_no}</td>
                        <td className="p-3 max-w-xs">
                          <div className="line-clamp-2 text-slate-800 font-medium">
                            <MathRenderer content={item.question_text} />
                          </div>
                        </td>
                        <td className="p-3 text-center font-bold text-indigo-700">
                          <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg">
                            {item.correct_option}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="p-3 text-center">
                          <div className="flex justify-center items-center space-x-1 font-mono text-[11px]">
                            {['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'].map((optKey) => {
                              const isCorrectKey = item.correct_option.includes(optKey)
                              const count = item.optionCounts?.[optKey] ?? 0
                              let pillStyle = 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border-slate-200'
                              if (isCorrectKey) pillStyle = 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 font-bold'
                              else if (optKey === item.topDistractorKey && count > 0) pillStyle = 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 border-rose-300 font-bold'
                              return (
                                <span key={optKey} className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded border ${pillStyle}`}>
                                  {optKey}:{count}
                                </span>
                              )
                            })}
                          </div>
                        </td>
                        <td className="p-3 text-center font-bold">
                          {item.topDistractorKey !== 'Tidak ada' ? (
                            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200 rounded text-[11px]">
                              Opsi {item.topDistractorKey} ({item.maxDistractorCount})
                            </span>
                          ) : (
